  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验从e+e-对撞中提取的特征禁闭尺度是否在强子环境（pp碰撞）中保持普适性，并为非微扰夸克碎裂提供清洁探针。Z标记喷注提供了比普通QCD喷注更纯净的夸克喷注初始条件，从而能够从可与e+e-数据对照的角度研究跨越禁闭相变的EEC行为。

Method: 将背对背Z+jet产生的TMD因子化框架与包含微扰演化和味道依赖非微扰碎裂函数的排斥性微分EEC喷注函数相结合。EEC喷注函数在NLL'精度下求值，夸克与胶子的非微扰尺度分别由现有e+e-和inclusive-jet数据约束。在sqrt(s)=8与13 TeV下对前向Z+jet在喷注pT区间(20,30)、(30,50)和(50,100) GeV给出预测。

Result: EEC分布作为z_chi的函数在小z_chi区域呈现有限平台，而相应的角分布表现出与禁闭相变相关的特征性翻转（turnover）结构。味道分解显示，随着喷注横动量增大，该相变越来越由夸克起始喷注主导。

Conclusion: 前向Z标记喷注为探测非微扰夸克碎裂提供了清洁途径，并可直接检验从e+e-碰撞提取的特征禁闭尺度是否在强子环境中具有普适性。

Abstract: We present predictions for the collinear energy-energy correlator (EEC) measured inside $Z$-tagged jets in proton-proton collisions across the confinement transition. Our framework combines transverse-momentum-dependent factorization for back-to-back $Z+$jet production with an exclusive differential EEC jet function incorporating perturbative evolution and flavor-dependent non-perturbative fragmentation. The EEC jet function is evaluated at $\mathrm{NLL}^{\prime}$ accuracy, with separate quark and gluon non-perturbative scales constrained by existing $e^+e^-$ and inclusive-jet data. We provide predictions for forward $Z+$jet production at $\sqrt{s}=8~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $13~\mathrm{TeV}$ in the jet transverse-momentum intervals $(20, 30)$, $(30, 50)$ and $(50, 100) \, \mathrm{GeV}$. The EEC distributions as functions of $z_χ$ exhibit finite plateaus at small $z_χ$, while the corresponding angular distributions display the characteristic turnover associated with the confinement transition. A flavor decomposition shows that this transition becomes increasingly dominated by quark-initiated jets as the jet transverse momentum increases. Forward $Z$-tagged jets therefore provide a clean probe of non-perturbative quark fragmentation and offer a direct test of whether the characteristic confinement scale extracted from $e^+e^-$ collisions remains universal in a hadronic environment.

</details>


### [101] [Quark Mixing Model with Three Higgs Doublets and $S_3$ Modular Symmetry at $τ=i$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03204)
*Carlos Cerón,Myriam Mondragón*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种补充了模S₃对称性的三希格斯电弱双重态模型中的夸克扇区。通过合理分配夸克和希格斯场在S₃下的表示及模权重，构建了现象学可行的质量矩阵纹理。结合标量势最小化给出的Higgs VEV条件和在不动点τ=i处的Yukawa耦合评估，大幅减少了自由参数。提出了两种方案：一种仅含三个有效参数（过度约束），另一种含四个有效参数（可精确拟合VCKM矩阵）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用模S₃对称性构建夸克质量与混合矩阵，探索是否可以通过对称性约束和Higgs势最小化条件减少参数数量，从而获得可预测且与实验数据吻合的夸克味结构。

Method: 在含三个Higgs电弱二重态的模型中引入模S₃对称性；对夸克和Higgs场在S₃下的表示及模权重进行特定赋值以生成质量矩阵纹理；利用标量势最小化推导Higgs VEV约束条件；在模空间不动点τ=i处评估Yukawa耦合，从而简化并减少自由参数。随后构建三种有效参数与四种有效参数两种方案进行数值拟合。

Result: 两种设置中，三有效参数的方案受到过度约束，难以完全匹配实验数据；而四有效参数的方案能够精确拟合VCKM矩阵，表明该模型具有现象学可行性。

Conclusion: 模S₃对称性的三Higgs双重态模型为夸克质量纹理提供了有前景的框架，但在参数数目最少化与实验可拟合性之间需保持平衡，四参数方案是可行的折中方案。

Abstract: We study the quark sector of a model with three Higgs electroweak doublets, complemented with a modular $S_3$ symmetry. We show that a proper assignment of the quark and Higgs fields in $S_3$ and their modular weights allows to write a mass matrix with a phenomenologically viable texture. Moreover, by considering the conditions on the Higgs VEVs derived from the minimization of the scalar potential together with evaluation of the Yukawa couplings at the fixed point $τ=i$, leads to a significant reduction of the free parameters. We present two setups, one with three effective parameters, which is overly constrained, and one with four effective parameters, where the $V_{CKM}$ matrix can be fitted precisely.

</details>


### [102] [Statistical Symmetry Breaking and Emergent Colored Noise in a Stochastic Scalar-Doublet Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03312)
*Pei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类相对论性随机场理论，其中复标量双重态与复白噪声源耦合。该作用在统计层面保持洛伦兹和U(1)×SU(2)对称性，但欧拉-拉格朗日方程在单条随机实现中表现出对称性破缺。作者在无规范场的扇区内获得了解析解，证明标量双重态在场空间中做噪声驱动的随机游走，其系综平均幅值随时间有限演化。进一步通过与费米子的Yukawa耦合，表明标量场解自然分裂为有效质量项（尾部）和诱导费米子波函数空间相关随机相位的色噪声源（光锥分量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索相对论性随机场理论中统计对称性与随机实现中的对称性破缺之间的关系，并研究随机效应如何产生涌现的色噪声动力学，为量子场论与随机过程的交叉领域提供新的理论视角。

Method: 构造一个复标量双重态与复白噪声源耦合的相对论性随机作用量，在保留统计层面U(1)×SU(2)与洛伦兹对称性的前提下，通过引入无规范场扇区简化方程以获得解析解，并利用Yukawa耦合将标量场解与费米子子系统连接起来，分析其产生的色噪声统计性质和相关长度。

Result: 证明了标量双重态在噪声驱动下发生场空间的随机游走，系综平均幅值随时间有限；标量场解分离出尾部（有效质量项）和光锥（色噪声源）两个分量；解析推导了费米子波函数中涌现色噪声的统计特征和相关长度。

Conclusion: 该工作为相对论性随机标量双重态场论中的统计对称性破缺和涌现色噪声动力学提供了探索性研究，揭示了线性化随机动力学可以在统计水平上保持对称性而在实现层面产生破缺，并通过Yukawa耦合将随机性传递给费米子子系统的机制。

Abstract: We investigate a relativistic stochastic field theory in which a complex scalar doublet is coupled to a complex white-noise source. The action preserves Lorentz and $\mathrm{U(1)}\times\mathrm{SU(2)}$ symmetries at the statistical level, whereas the corresponding Euler-Lagrange equations exhibit symmetry breaking along individual stochastic realizations. Within a gauge-field-free sector introduced to obtain analytical solutions, we show that the scalar doublet undergoes a noise-driven random walk in field space, leading to a finite, time-dependent ensemble average of its magnitude. As an illustrative application, we further investigate the coupling of the stochastic scalar field to fermions through a Yukawa interaction. The scalar-field solution naturally separates into a tail component, which contributes as an effective mass-like term, and a light-cone component, which acts as a colored-noise source that induces a spatially correlated stochastic phase in the fermion wave function. The statistical properties and correlation length of this emergent colored noise are derived analytically within the adopted approximations. The present work provides an exploratory study of statistical symmetry breaking and emergent colored-noise dynamics in a relativistic stochastic scalar-doublet field theory.

</details>


### [103] [Leading-Neutron Electroproduction at HERA and the EIC: Sullivan Process, Target Fragmentation, and Pion PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03368)
*Wen-Chen Chang,Chia-Yu Hsieh,Satyajit Puhan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用Pythia事件生成器对HERA实验的领头中子电产生数据中的靶碎裂贡献进行建模，结合Sullivan单π交换框架，证明二者组合可在完整x_L范围内重现数据特征而无需额外归一化因子，从而为未来全局分析纳入更广范围数据以扩展对小x_π的灵敏度铺平道路，并给出了EIC上的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对HERA领头中子数据的分析主要集中于大x_L区域（DIS靶碎裂贡献被抑制），并通过Sullivan单π交换框架约束π部分子分布函数。然而，若能将数据范围扩展到全部x_L区间，则可进一步提升对小x_π的灵敏度。为此需要正确处理靶碎裂贡献，这是此前分析中被忽略或需要额外归一化因子的关键问题。

Method: 使用Pythia事件生成器对DIS靶碎裂贡献进行建模，并将其与Sullivan单π交换框架下的OPE贡献组合，在全x_L范围内与HERA H1和ZEUS领头中子电产生数据进行比较；同时考察π-核子顶点形状因子的模型依赖性以及Pythia中不同靶碎裂处理方案的影响，并给出未来EIC动能构型下的预测。

Result: (1) OPE贡献与Pythia靶碎裂贡献的组合可在完整x_L范围内无需额外归一化因子即可重现HERA领头中子数据的主要特征；(2) HERA数据对不同靶碎裂处理方案表现出敏感性；(3) 识别出EIC上具有增强π结构灵敏度的束流能量构型和运动学区域。

Conclusion: 这项工作表明，通过Pythia建模靶碎裂贡献，可将更广范围的HERA领头中子数据纳入未来的全局拟合中，从而扩展对小x_π的灵敏度；同时为EIC上的领头中子测量提供了优化指导，有望进一步约束π部分子分布函数。

Abstract: Leading-neutron electroproduction measurements from the H1 and ZEUS experiments at HERA have been used to constrain pion parton distribution functions (PDFs) at small momentum fractions within the Sullivan one-pion-exchange (OPE) framework, complementing large-$x_π$ constraints from pion-induced Drell--Yan measurements. Previous analyses have focused primarily on the region of large neutron longitudinal momentum fraction, $x_L$, where contributions from deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) target fragmentation are suppressed. In this work, we use the \textsc{Pythia} event generator to model the target-fragmentation contribution and show that its combination with the OPE contribution reproduces the main features of the HERA leading-neutron data over the full measured $x_L$ range without introducing additional ad hoc normalization factors. This result demonstrates the potential of incorporating a broader range of leading-neutron data into future global analyses, thereby extending sensitivity to smaller pion momentum fractions $x_π$. We investigate the model dependence associated with the pion--nucleon vertex form factor and show that the HERA data are sensitive to different target-fragmentation treatments implemented in \textsc{Pythia}. Finally, we present projections for leading-neutron production at the future U.S. Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), identifying beam-energy configurations and kinematic regions that provide enhanced sensitivity to pion structure while suppressing DIS target-fragmentation contributions.

</details>


### [104] [Meson mass spectrum in isospin QCD medium from the $N_f=2+1$ quark-meson model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03385)
*Yu-Han Gao,Xu-Guang Huang,Daiki Suenaga*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限温度和密度下研究具有同位旋化学势$μ_I$的QCD相结构、介子质量谱和声速。采用包含Kobayashi-Maskawa-'t Hooft型耦合的$N_f=2+1$夸克-介子模型，在平均场近似下分析了π凝聚的起始，发现低温下表现为二级相变，高温下可表现为一级相变。系统分析了标量/赝标量单重态-八重态介子质量谱，其中π凝聚超流相中π_+由于Nambu-Goldstone玻色子本质严格无质量，中性π介子质量随$μ_I$呈严格线性增长。研究还发现等温声速平方存在与相变相关的特征结构，且增强的$U(1)_A$反常效应使冷介质中π凝聚产生的声速峰值变得不那么显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD在有限同位旋化学势下π凝聚物质的相结构、介子激发谱（尤其是Nambu-Goldstone模式的质量行为）以及输运性质（声速），为未来格点QCD模拟提供对称性方面的参考信息。

Method: 采用$N_f=2+1$夸克-介子模型，引入Kobayashi-Maskawa-'t Hooft型耦合以描述$U(1)_A$轴性反常的动力学效应；在平均场近似和夸克单圈近似下计算有效势，分析π凝聚相变行为，并推导标量与赝标量介子的质量谱以及等温声速平方。

Result: π凝聚在低温下为二级相变，高温下在该近似框架内可能出现一级相变；π_+介子在超流相中严格无质量（NG玻色子），中性π介子质量在凝聚相中随$μ_I$严格线性增长；等温声速平方呈现与相变对应的特征结构；增强$U(1)_A$反常效应会削弱冷介质中π凝聚引起的声速峰。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了同位旋化学势下π凝聚物质的相结构、介子质量谱和声速的sysmmetry驱动行为，尤其是$U(1)_A$反常对声速峰值的调制作用，可为未来格点模拟提供对称性视角的有用预测信息。

Abstract: We study phase structures, meson mass spectra, and sound velocities at finite temperature and density in QCD with isospin chemical potential ($μ_I$). We employ the quark-meson model with $N_f=2+1$, incorporating the Kobayashi-Maskawa-'t Hooft type coupling to capture dynamical effects of the $U(1)_A$ axial anomaly. Within the mean-field approximation at quark one-loop, we analyze the onset of pion condensation, which manifests as a second-order phase transition at low temperatures and may exhibit a first-order behavior at higher temperatures within this approximation. We examine the corresponding mass spectra of scalar and pseudoscalar singlet-octet mesons, in which the $π_+$ mass is exactly massless in the superfluid phase due to its Nambu-Goldstone boson nature. The neutral pion mass is, meanwhile, found to exhibit a strictly linear growth with $μ_I$ in the pion condensed phase. We also investigate the isothermal squared sound velocity and identify characteristic structures associated with the phase transitions. Furthermore, we highlight how enhanced $U(1)_A$ anomaly effects facilitate the pion condensate to generate a less-pronounced sound velocity peak in cold medium. Our findings are expected to provide future lattice simulations with useful information on meson mass spectra from symmetry aspects.

</details>


### [105] [Exploring millicharged particles in laboratory and astrophysical strong-field regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03399)
*Cheng-Rui Jiang,Tong Li,Kai Ma,Haolong Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强电磁场环境中探测和约束轻质量带毫电荷粒子（MCPs）的可能性，涵盖实验室激光实验和磁星天体物理环境。作者提出通过相对论电子束与高强度激光脉冲相互作用产生MCP对（非线性康普顿散射），并使用Furry绘景和Volkov解计算截面；同时重新审视了磁星极隙中通过Schwinger机制产生MCP对的过程，发现来自强磁化磁星的约束与实验室激光实验的搜索潜力具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越标准模型（SM）的轻暗物质粒子在强场环境下的探测具有重要意义，尤其是轻质量毫荷粒子（MCPs）作为暗物质候选者或连接暗物质扇区的媒介，其探测和约束对理解超出标准模型的新物理至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出利用非线性康普顿散射（相对论电子束与高强激光脉冲相互作用）产生MCP对；2) 采用Furry绘景和Dirac方程在外场中的Volkov解描述电磁背景中的电子与MCP；3) 计算MCP对的非线性康普顿散射截面，并考虑含中微子丢失的不可约SM背景；4) 基于Ruderman-Sutherland模型重新审视磁星极隙中强磁场和平行电场下的Schwinger机制MCP对产生；5) 评估MCP的Schwinger对产生与电场加速导致的能量损失。

Result: 研究发现，来自强磁化磁星的约束与实验室激光实验的搜索潜力具有互补性，表明两种途径可协同用于探索轻质量MCPs的参数空间。

Conclusion: 实验室强激光实验与天体物理磁星环境为探测轻质量毫荷粒子提供了互补且有效的约束和搜索途径，未来可在多个方向上协同推进对超出标准模型轻粒子的探索。

Abstract: The probe of light dark particles beyond the Standard Model (SM) under a strong-field environment has drawn significant attention. In this work, we investigate the potential to search for and constrain light millicharged particles (MCPs) via strong electromagnetic fields in both laboratory laser experiments and astrophysical environments such as magnetars. We propose the MCP pair production from nonlinear Compton scattering through the interaction of a relativistic electron beam with a high-intensity laser pulse. The Furry picture and Volkov solution of Dirac equation in a background electromagnetic field are used to describe the electrons and MCPs under an external classical laser field. We calculate the cross sections of nonlinear Compton scattering to MCP pairs and take into account the irreducible SM background with missing neutrinos. We also revisit the MCP pair production via the Schwinger mechanism from magnetars with ultra-strong magnetic field and parallel electric field in polar gap. The energy loss due to the Schwinger pair production of MCPs and electric field acceleration is evaluated based on Ruderman-Sutherland model for confirmed magnetars. We find that the constraints from highly magnetized magnetars and the search potential in laboratory laser experiments are complementary.

</details>


### [106] [CGC-py: A Monte Carlo Event Generator for Gluon Saturation Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03434)
*Haowu Duan,Cong Yi,Si-Wei Dai,Shu-Yi Wei,Wenbin Zhao,Liang Zheng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍CGC-py蒙特卡洛事件生成器，耦合完整的色玻璃凝聚（CGC）深非弹散射截面与部分子分支横动量依赖的末态后向初态簇射（由Pythia 8处理末态辐射和强子化），保留靶弹性与非弹性全部贡献，不取背对背关联极限，从而可在同一事件样本中一致性生成单强子与双强子观测量。通过解析闭环检验与H1实验数据对比验证，并预测了饱和模式下的核修正因子及能量依赖的awayside展宽特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件生成器在处理小x玻色子-胶子融合动力学与饱和效应时存在局限，作者旨在构建一个能同时、自洽地描述ep与eA深非弹散射中单/双强子观测量、并敏感反映小x胶子饱和动力学的完整MC生成器，服务于EIC物理研究。

Method: 将完整CGC截面对γ*p(A)→qq̄+X与Parton-Branching横动量依赖后向初态簇射耦合，Pythia 8负责末态辐射与强子化；不取背对背关联极限，保留靶弹性与非弹性全部贡献。采用analytic closure test验证单非包含夸克谱，并与H1电子-质子的带电强子谱对比。

Result: 与H1数据比较取得极好一致；预测eAu的核修正因子R_eAu^h呈现低p_T抑制、随p_T升高趋近1的饱和模式；对比Pythia 6基线与x_g重标度研究表明，小x CGC演化和共线DGLAP动力学对双强子awayside宽度随能量的增长贡献相当，纯饱和驱动的展宽仅出现在最高能量下。eAu相对ep展示增大的awayside宽度和压低的反向产额，但这些核效应在EIC运动学范围内仍较温和。

Conclusion: CGC-py是自洽且经验证有效的CGC饱和动力学事件生成器。EIC标准运动学下核效应对饱和敏感度有限，建议在最前向可达运动学区间开展测量，并结合互补观测量以最大化对胶子饱和的灵敏度。

Abstract: We develop CGC-py, a Monte Carlo event generator for deep-inelastic scattering. It couples the full Color Glass Condensate (CGC) cross section for $γ^*p(A)\to q\bar q+X$ to a Parton-Branching transverse-momentum-dependent backward initial-state shower, while \textsc{Pythia}~8 handles final-state radiation and hadronization. CGC-py retains the complete target-elastic and target-inelastic contributions without taking the back-to-back correlation limit, allowing single- and di-hadron observables to be generated consistently from the same event sample. We validate the generator through an analytic closure test of the single-inclusive quark spectrum and a comparison of charged-hadron spectra in $ep$ collisions with H1 data, finding excellent agreement. The predicted nuclear modification factor $R_{e\mathrm{Au}}^h$ shows the expected saturation pattern: suppression at low $p_T^*$ followed by a rise toward unity at higher $p_T^*$. A comparison with a \textsc{Pythia}~6 baseline, together with an $x_g$-rescaling study, indicates that small-$x$ CGC evolution and collinear DGLAP dynamics contribute comparably to the growth of the dihadron away-side width with energy. Genuine saturation-driven broadening emerges only at the highest energies considered. Within CGC-py, $e\mathrm{Au}$ collisions exhibit an enhanced away-side width and a suppressed back-to-back yield relative to $ep$ collisions. These nuclear effects remain modest over EIC kinematics, motivating measurements at the most forward accessible kinematics and the use of complementary observables to maximize sensitivity to gluon saturation.

</details>


### [107] [A Bayesian Pixel Based Approach for Model Independent TMD Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03646)
*Marco Zaccheddu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于非参数像素的贝叶斯框架，用于横向动量依赖（TMD）部分子分布函数的推断与成像。该方法将TMD演化整合进Collins-Soper-Sterman形式的可微框架中，并利用生成式AI（混合归一化流驱动的Metropolis-Hastings算法）实现高效后验采样。通过多尺度闭合检验验证框架有效性，并利用奇异值分解表征空TMD（即观测无法约束的功能成分），证明多尺度数据可打破这些简并性，实现三维部分子成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMD部分子分布函数是描述质子三维内部结构（横动量与纵向动量）的关键量子色动力学量，但其从实验数据中的提取面临严重挑战：传统参数化方法受限于预定义的函数形式，可能引入模型偏差；且TMD的重构问题存在固有简并性（空方向），即以当前观测数据无法唯一约束某些功能成分。因此需要一种非参数、可扩展且能处理不确定性的推断框架来克服这些局限。

Method: 1) 采用非参数像素化方法表示TMD，避免固定函数形式的先验限制；2) 将CSS形式的TMD演化整合进可微分编程框架，支持梯度计算；3) 设计混合归一化流（normalizing flow）驱动的Metropolis-Hastings采样算法，利用生成式AI加速后验空间探索；4) 通过逐步增加复杂度的多尺度闭合检验验证方法的统计一致性与数值稳定性；5) 使用奇异值分解（SVD）系统分析观测算子是否存在零空间，从而识别空TMD成分并研究多尺度数据如何打破简并性。

Result: 框架通过了从简单到复杂设置的多尺度闭合检验。SVD分析揭示了空TMD的存在——即那些在给定观测集合下不受约束的TMD功能方向。进一步证明随着多尺度数据的引入，这些简并方向逐渐被打破，使得原本不可观测的TMD成分得以被约束，从而实现了玻色子内三维动量空间的部分子成像。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了结合贝叶斯非参数推断、可微物理建模与生成式AI的三者融合，为TMD部分子分布的可靠提取提供了新范式。多尺度数据的互补性对于克服TMD重构中的简并性至关重要，而SVD分析为理解哪些TMD方向可被观测所约束提供了系统诊断工具。这一框架为未来在实验数据上实现真正的高精度三维部分子成像奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a nonparametric pixel-based framework for the Bayesian inference and imaging of transverse momentum dependent (TMD) parton distributions. The methodology integrates TMD evolution within the Collins-Soper-Sterman formalism in a differentiable framework, and leverages generative AI through a hybrid normalizing flow-driven Metropolis-Hastings algorithm for efficient posterior sampling. The framework is validated through multi-scale closure tests of increasing complexity. Using singular value decomposition, we characterize the existence of null TMDs, functional components that remain unconstrained by observables, and demonstrate how multi-scale data break these degeneracies, enabling 3D partonic imaging.

</details>


### [108] [Light Leptoquarks in a Dark Sector: Scalar Dark Matter, Neutrino Mass, and Collider Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03648)
*Priyotosh Bandyopadhyay,Kirtiman Ghosh,Anirban Karan,Snehashis Parashar,Debabrata Sahoo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个包含暗轻子夸克(LQ)、暗矢量夸克(VLQ)和标量单态粒子的暗轻子夸克模型中的暗物质现象学及对撞机信号。该模型通过一圈图辐射产生马约拉纳中微子质量。由于带色的LQ和VLQ不能作为暗物质候选者，引入Z2奇标量单态作为暗物质。LQ的Z2奇性禁止其传统的夸克-轻子衰变，使其逃逸标准LHC限制，从而实现亚TeV暗LQ与TeV级暗VLQ共存的可能性，并通过共湮灭机制获得观测到的丰度。论文还评估了未来缪子对撞机的探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能够同时解释暗物质和中微子质量的模型，特别是寻找可以在未来缪子对撞机上被探测到的新物理信号。标准LHC对可见LQ的约束使得传统LQ模型受到严格限制，因此需要利用Z2奇性来规避这些约束，拓展参数空间。

Method: 构建一个包含暗LQ、暗VLQ和Z2奇标量单态的理论框架，其中暗LQ和VLQ参与一圈图辐射产生马约拉纳中微子质量。由于Z2对称性限制了LQ的常规衰变通道，使其不受传统LHC约束，进而系统分析暗物质丰度（通过共湮灭机制）、暗物质现象学以及在未来缪子对撞机上的探测可能性。

Result: 该框架允许亚TeV质量的暗LQ与TeV级暗VLQ共存，前者通过共湮灭帮助实现标量单态暗物质正确的剩余丰度。研究给出了暗物质现象学的结果，并评估了未来缪子对撞机对该场景的探测能力。

Conclusion: 该暗LQ模型为中微子质量和暗物质提供了统一解释，同时因Z2奇性规避了标准LHC对LQ的约束，为未来缪子对撞机提供了独特且可探测的信号前景。

Abstract: We investigate the dark matter (DM) phenomenology and subsequent collider signatures of a dark leptoquark (LQ) model containing dark vector-like quarks (VLQs) and a scalar singlet. The dark LQs and VLQs participate in the radiative generation of Majorana neutrino masses at one loop. Since the coloured LQs and VLQs cannot serve as viable DM candidates, a $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd singlet scalar is introduced as the DM candidate. The $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd nature of the LQs forbids their conventional decays into a quark and a lepton, allowing them to evade the standard LHC constraints that apply to visible LQ signatures. This framework therefore offers the distinctive possibility of sub-TeV dark LQs coexisting with TeV-scale dark VLQs. These sub-TeV dark LQs help in achieving the observed relic abundance of the singlet through co-annihilation. We analyse the resulting DM phenomenology and assess the prospects for probing this scenario at a future muon collider.

</details>


### [109] [Probing the Geometry of Viable Froggatt-Nielsen-like Flavor Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03812)
*Davide Meloni*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在受控的玩具地貌中研究可行的Froggatt-Nielsen类味结构几何，发现原始指数空间中不存在强线性压缩——所有基准中需要15个主成分解释90%方差，最近邻本征维度估计约为10-12，表明原始Froggatt-Nielsen指数并非揭示味理论背后几何的自然坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 味物理学中Froggatt-Nielsen模型提供了解释夸克质量层级和CKM混合矩阵的理论框架，但其风味纹理的参数空间几何结构尚未被系统研究。作者希望通过首次"纹理级分析"探索可行的Froggatt-Nielsen类味结构是否在原始指数空间中展现出低维几何结构。

Method: 每个点由上下型Yukawa矩阵的18个整数指数表示，不施加具体Froggatt-Nielsen模型的电荷因子化约束。通过夸克质量比和CKM可观测量设定可行性截断，构建四个从质量约束到完整CKM可观测量的基准集成，并在原始指数空间中使用主成分分析（PCA）与最近邻本征维度诊断（TwoNN和Levina-Bickel估计器）分析点云结构。

Result: 所有基准中需要15个主成分才能解释90%的方差，表明不存在强线性压缩。TwoNN和Levina-Bickel估计器给出约10-12的高有效维度，支持同一结论。结果不排除味理论几何存在于其他坐标中，但表明原始Froggatt-Nielsen指数可能不是揭示此类几何的自然坐标。

Conclusion: 原始Froggatt-Nielsen指数空间中的可行解呈现出高维分布特性，未发现隐藏的低维味结构，这提示未来研究应探索其他坐标或更自然的参数化方式来揭示潜在的味理论几何。

Abstract: We investigate the geometry of viable Froggatt--Nielsen-like flavor textures in a controlled toy landscape. In this first texture-level analysis, each point is represented by the eighteen integer exponents entering the up- and down-type Yukawa matrices, without imposing the additional charge-factorization constraints of specific Froggatt--Nielsen models. Viability is imposed through cuts on quark mass ratios and selected CKM observables. We construct four benchmark ensembles, ranging from masses-only constraints to the inclusion of the full set of CKM observables considered in this work, and study the resulting point clouds in the raw exponent space. Principal-component analysis shows no evidence for strong linear compression: in all benchmarks, fifteen principal components are required to account for 90% of the variance. Nearest-neighbour intrinsic-dimensionality diagnostics, including TwoNN and Levina--Bickel estimators, support the same qualitative conclusion, yielding high effective dimensions of order 10-12. These results do not rule out hidden flavor-theory geometry in other coordinates; rather, they suggest that raw Froggatt--Nielsen exponents may not provide natural coordinates for revealing such a geometry.

</details>


### [110] [Exploring Extended Higgs Dynamics via Higgsstrahlung at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04039)
*Anisha,Francisco Arco,Stefano Di Noi,Christoph Englert,Margarete Mühlleitner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来正负电子对撞机FCC-ee在240 GeV运行下，通过Z玻色子伴随的Higgs产生过程，在双Higgs二重态模型（2HDM）框架内以次领头阶精度约束超越标准模型（BSM）的Higgs扇区相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC-ee将以前所未有的精度探测Higgs玻色子和电弱尺度，但需要一个具体且小参数空间的理论框架来评估其约束能力。2HDM作为最简单的BSM扩展之一，其关键特征（新标量态、Higgs混合、Higgs间非平凡耦合）也普遍存在于更复杂的扩展模型中，因此通过2HDM展示FCC-ee的间接发现潜力具有重要意义。

Method: 以2HDM为框架，在next-to-leading order（NLO）精度下计算e+e-→ZH过程的截面，研究对齐极限（alignment limit）附近BSM相关Higgs扇区相互作用如何偏离标准模型预期，并评估FCC-ee在240 GeV运行的投影精度对这一偏离的敏感度。

Result: 研究表明，FCC-ee在240 GeV运行的投影精度将能够对对齐极限附近的BSM相关Higgs扇区相互作用进行精确分析，即能够敏锐地探测到2HDM中由新标量态存在、Higgs混合和非平凡Higgs间耦合所导致的截面偏离。

Conclusion: 由于2HDM中驱动截面偏离标准模型预期的关键特征在更复杂的BSM扩展中也普遍存在，因此本文结果展示了FCC-ee通过精确测量ZH产生过程，具有在电弱尺度间接揭示BSM物理的强大潜力。

Abstract: The Future Circular Electron-Positron Collider (FCC-ee) will probe aspects of the Higgs boson and the electroweak scale with unprecedented precision via associated Higgs production with a $Z$ boson. We focus on the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM) to frame the FCC-ee's precision constraints within a small, concrete parameter space at next-to-leading order. We demonstrate that the projected precision of the FCC-ee's 240 GeV run will enable a precise analysis of Beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM)-relevant Higgs-sector interactions near the alignment limit. As the key aspects of the 2HDM that drive deviations of the cross section from its Standard Model expectation (presence of new scalar states, Higgs mixing, and non-trivial inter-Higgs couplings) are also typically present in extensions more complex than the 2HDM, this demonstrates the FCC-ee's indirect potential for unveiling BSM physics around the electroweak scale.

</details>


### [111] [Quasicrystalline Inflation: From the Cosmological Dipole to Primordial Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02142)
*Jiro Soda*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"准晶暴胀"概念，指出六种准周期相位可分解为三个声子与三个相位子模式。纯相位子位移可产生局域电偶极子而线性无迹四极子为零。基于空间变化的二次有效场论，推导出动量转移被限制在六个二十面体波矢整数组合范围内的非对角关联，构成原初准周期序（原初衍射）的特征观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宇宙暴胀阶段可能存在的准周期序结构，寻找超越标准暴胀模型的、具有特征性可观测信号的原始物理机制。

Method: 提出六种准周期相位分解为三个声子和三个相位子；利用相干纯相位子位移产生局域电偶极子（线性无迹四极矩消失）；通过空间变化的二次有效场论推导非对角关联函数，其动量转移限制在六个二十面体波矢的整数组合内。

Result: 非对角关联信号被限制在二十面体准周期波矢的整数组合上，产生特征性的"原初衍射"模式的观测迹线。

Conclusion: 这些受限的非对角关联信号可作为探测原初宇宙中准周期序的独特观测签名，为区分不同暴胀模型提供新途径。

Abstract: We propose quasicrystalline inflation. Six quasiperiodic phases decompose into three phonons and three phasons. It turns out that a coherent pure-phason displacement can generate a local power dipole while the linear traceless quadrupole vanishes. From spatially varying quadratic effective field theory, we derive off-diagonal correlations whose momentum transfer is restricted to integer combinations of the six icosahedral wavevectors. These correlated off-diagonal signals provide a characteristic observational signature of primordial quasiperiodic order, primordial diffraction.

</details>


### [112] [TQ4Q2.0 Fragmentation Functions for Fully Heavy Tetraquark Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04049)
*Francesco Giovanni Celiberto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了TQ4Q2.0碎裂函数集，用于研究全重四夸克态（标量0++、轴矢量1+-和张量2++）的产生动力学。碎裂输入基于NRQCD因子化框架确定，并通过HF-NRevo方案跨越重味阈值进行演化，综合了碎裂标度变化的微扰不确定性与色复合长距离矩阵元中的非微扰效应。轴矢量扇区对LHC和FCC能量下的内禀粲贡献尤为敏感。TQ4Q2.0为宽运动学范围内全重四夸克产生提供了含有不确定性评估的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全重四夸克态的产生机制长期以来缺乏系统且具有完整不确定性控制的碎裂函数框架，以往研究未充分覆盖不同量子数配置（标量、轴矢量和张量）的碎裂动力学，也未能同时处理微扰与非微扰效应的自洽传播。

Method: 在NRQCD因子化框架下确定完整部分子道集合的碎裂输入，涵盖0++、1+-和2++三种量子数配置；通过HF-NRevo方案将碎裂函数跨越重味阈值进行演化；系统传播由碎裂标度变化引起的微扰不确定性，并整合编码于色复合长距离矩阵元中的非微扰效应。

Result: 建立了TQ4Q2.0碎裂函数集，揭示了轴矢量扇区对固有粲（intrinsic charm）贡献在LHC和FCC能量下的高敏感性，并在宽运动学范围内实现了全重四夸克产生的不确定性感知描述。

Conclusion: TQ4Q2.0为全重四夸克产生提供了系统性的、带有不确定度评估的碎裂框架，有望将奇特强子谱学与质子的部分子结构联系起来。

Abstract: We investigate the fragmentation dynamics underlying the production of fully heavy tetraquarks by means of the TQ4Q2.0 collinear FF set, covering scalar ($0^{++}$), axial-vector ($1^{+-}$), and tensor ($2^{++}$) configurations. The fragmentation inputs are determined within NRQCD factorization for the complete set of relevant partonic channels and are subsequently evolved across heavy-flavor thresholds through the HF-NRevo scheme. Perturbative uncertainties associated with fragmentation-scale variations are consistently propagated together with nonperturbative effects encoded in color-composite long-distance matrix elements. Attention is devoted to the axial-vector sector, whose fragmentation pattern makes it especially sensitive to intrinsic-charm contributions at LHC and FCC energies. TQ4Q2.0 therefore provides an uncertainty-aware framework for exploring fully heavy tetraquark production over a broad kinematic range and for connecting exotic-hadron spectroscopy with the partonic structure of the proton.

</details>


### [113] [Inelastic Signatures of Electroweak Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04144)
*Juri Smirnov,Spencer Griffith,John F. Beacom*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨由Majorana和Dirac多重态通过Higgs耦合构成的最小电弱暗物质模型，该框架可解释LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ)合作组最近报道的高能非弹性核反冲事件。在custodial对称点，电弱对称性破缺产生中性态劈裂δ和离对角Z相互作用。对于耦合多重态3M2D、5M4D等，劈裂和领先阶非弹性相互作用在固定(mχ,y)下是普适的。观测到的高反冲能量指向几百keV的劈裂，Z介导的固定速率允许确定质量依赖的δLZ(mχ)，从而在(mχ,y)平面定义普适区域。与表示依赖的热遗迹轨迹相交可为每个多重态选择基准点，相应的电弱表示预言在更低反冲能量处存在关联的圈诱导弹性自旋无关信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ合作组最近报道了一种可能与暗物质直接探测相关的高能非弹性核反冲事件，需要一个理论上可预测的框架来解释这类事件。传统的电弱多重态暗物质模型多关注弹性散射，而本文旨在利用Majorana和Dirac多重态耦合的Higgs相互作用，建立能产生所需劈裂和非弹性散射的最小电弱框架，以解释LZ观测并同时满足热遗迹丰度约束。

Method: 作者考虑由一个Majorana多重态和一个Dirac多重态通过Higgs相互作用耦合的暗物质模型。在custodial点上分析电弱对称性破缺如何诱导中性态质量劈裂和离对角Z玻色子相互作用。对于一系列多重态组合(3M2D, 5M4D, 7M6D, 9M8D, 11M10D, 13M12D)，发现劈裂与领先非弹性散射在固定参数(mχ,y)下是普适的（不依赖电弱表示）。通过要求LZ曝光中预期观察到一次非弹性事件，结合两边的90%泊松带，确定质量依赖的劈裂δLZ(mχ)，从而在(mχ,y)平面定义普适区域，再与依赖于表示的热遗迹轨迹相交选择基准点。

Result: 该框架能够解释LZ报道的高能反冲事件，所需的劈裂量为几百keV量级。在(mχ,y)参数平面上确定了普适区域，并成功为每种多重态组合找到与热遗迹约束一致的基准参数点。此外，每个选定的电弱表示预言了圈诱导的弹性自旋无关信号，该信号出现在较低的反冲能量区域，与LZ观测到的非弹性事件互补，可作为未来验证该模型的实验探针。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一个电弱多重态暗物质模型，其中Majorana和Dirac多重态间的Higgs耦合可自然地解释LZ实验最新报道的高能非弹性反冲事件。该框架在固定参数下显示出不依赖于电弱表示的多重态普适性，同时每个表示还给出可检验的弹性自旋无关预言，为未来的直接探测实验提供了明确的验证途径。

Abstract: Minimal electroweak dark matter (DM) extended by a Majorana (M) and a Dirac (D) multiplet coupled through Higgs interactions provides a predictive framework for high-energy inelastic nuclear recoils. We show that this setup can account for the type of event recently reported by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration. At the custodial point, electroweak symmetry breaking induces a neutral-state splitting $δ$ and an off-diagonal $Z$ interaction. For the coupled multiplets $3_M2_D$, $5_M4_D$, $7_M6_D$, $9_M8_D$, $11_M10_D$, and $13_M12_D$, both the splitting and the leading inelastic interaction are universal at fixed $(m_χ,y)$, independent of the electroweak representation. The observed high recoil energy points to splittings of a few hundred keV, while the fixed $Z$-mediated rate allows us to determine a mass-dependent $δ_{\rm LZ}(m_χ)$ by requiring one expected inelastic event in the LZ exposure, with a two-sided $90\%$ Poisson band. This defines a universal region in the $(m_χ,y)$ plane. Intersecting it with the representation-dependent thermal relic trajectories selects a benchmark for each multiplet. The corresponding electroweak representation then predicts a correlated loop-induced elastic spin-independent signal at lower recoil energies.

</details>


### [114] [TeV Higgsino Interpretation of the LZ High-Recoil Event with Intermediate-Scale Electroweak Gauginos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04163)
*Xiaokang Du,Fei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对LUX-ZEPLIN实验报告的高反冲事件，提出了一个TeV级Higgsino暗物质候选者，其拥有亚MeV级的中性态质量劈裂。作者证明在SU(5) GUT尺度下非普适边界条件M1^G/M2^G=-3/5能够在MSSM中抵消bino和wino的领先贡献，并通过均匀单圈演化保持不变，从而在Higgsino尺度下实现所需的极小能隙。文章进一步给出了wino质量约120 TeV、缝隙约350 keV、Higgsino质量1.091 TeV、tanβ=10的受控树级解，并识别了能实现所需gaugino比例混合SU(5)表示，同时指出该构造下的非弹性Z耦合仍未被压制，全密度热Higgsino依然受太阳俘获边界约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN实验观察到的极端高反冲事件暗示可能存在TeV级Higgsino暗物质，其具有亚MeV的中性态劈裂。然而在MSSM中如此小的质量差在远离抵消的情况下要求电弱gaugino质量高达10^7 GeV量级，远超Higgsino质量，这对模型构建构成严峻挑战。作者旨在寻找一种可控的理论框架，使这种极小能隙与TeV级Higgsino在自然的参数区域内得以实现。

Method: 作者采用SU(5) GUT框架下的非普适边界条件M1^G/M2^G=-3/5，从理论上证明该比值能在单圈均匀演化下保持不变，从而在Higgsino尺度抵消bino和wino的领先辐射贡献。对于剩余的有限gaugino项，作者以wino质量约120 TeV、能隙约350 keV、Higgsino质量1.091 TeV和tanβ=10作为输入条件，寻求可控的树级解。同时识别了能实现所需gaugino质量比的混合SU(5)表示，并对辐射敏感性和能谱一致性进行了检查。最后还评估了非弹性Z耦合在太阳俘获界限下的影响。

Result: 作者成功论证了在SU(5) GUT边界采用M1^G/M2^G=-3/5的条件可以精确抵消导致极小能隙的bino与wino领先贡献，且该比值在均匀单圈演化下保持不变。在所考察的参数点（wino约120 TeV，缝隙350 keV，Higgsino 1.091 TeV，tanβ=10）上得到了自洽的树级解。然而，由于非弹性Z耦合基本未被压制，该构造下的热Higgsino仍受到已有太阳俘获搜索的强约束。

Conclusion: 该工作为LUX-ZEPLIN高反冲事件提供了一种MSSM内的理论解释路径，展示了GUT边界非普适条件如何天然产生亚MeV量级的中性态劈裂。但文章同时指出，该参数区在许多星系与输运假设下仍与太阳俘获约束冲突，因此这一构造尽管在质量谱上自洽，其暗物质可行性仍受实验与天体物理边界的限制。

Abstract: The high-recoil event reported by LUX--ZEPLIN motivates a TeV Higgsino with a sub-MeV neutral-state splitting. Away from cancellations, such a small gap in the MSSM points to electroweak-gaugino masses of order $10^7\,\mathrm{GeV}$, far above the Higgsino mass. We show that the non-universal boundary condition $M_1^G/M_2^G=-3/5$ at the $\mathrm{SU}(5)$ GUT scale cancels the leading bino and wino contributions and is preserved by homogeneous one-loop evolution down to the Higgsino scale. The remaining finite-gaugino terms admit a controlled tree-level solution with a wino mass near $120\TeV$ for a $350\keV$ gap, a $1.091\TeV$ Higgsino and $\tanβ=10$. We identify mixed $\mathrm{SU}(5)$ representations that realize the required gaugino ratio and examine the radiative sensitivity and spectrum consistency of this construction. Because the inelastic $Z$ coupling remains essentially unsuppressed, a full-density thermal Higgsino is still subject to the published solar-capture bounds under their astrophysical and transport assumptions.

</details>


### [115] [Confronting the Higgsino Interpretation of the LZ Event with the High-Energy Sideband](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04175)
*Nicholas L. Rodd,Benjamin R. Safdi,Tracy R. Slatyer,Weishuang Linda Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了LZ实验观测到的单个248 keV核反冲事件，探讨了该事件是否由非弹性散射暗物质（特别是热产生的1.1 TeV higgsino）解释。作者发现该解释会产生更多高能侧带反冲事件，与LZ在S1c能量轴上高能区零事件的观测存在潜在张力；非热学~500 GeV higgsino可以规避此约束，未来重靶实验可判定该解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了一个248 keV的单核反冲事件，而低能区无反冲事件的现象与非弹性散射暗物质的特征一致，且这个事件似乎与热产生的1.1 TeV higgsino暗物质模型吻合。作者希望检验该解释是否与LZ在其他能量区间的观测兼容。

Method: 作者对比了非弹性散射higgsino暗物质模型预测的核反冲能谱与LZ实验在S1c能量轴高能侧带中的零事件观测，检验该模型的兼容性；并进一步考察非热产生的~500 GeV higgsino能否避开该约束。

Result: 热产生的1.1 TeV higgsino暗物质模型预计会在高能侧带产生额外反冲事件，而LZ在该能量区报告零事件，两者存在潜在张力；非热学~500 GeV higgsino可以规避该侧带约束。

Conclusion: LZ实验的248 keV事件是否是higgsino暗物质的结果仍存疑问；该解释在高能侧带与LZ数据存在张力，亟需LZ合作组公布该能量区的接受度以进一步验证，未来使用重靶的实验可帮助裁定该解释。

Abstract: The LZ experiment has reported a single 248 keV nuclear recoil event in their $2.84$ ton-yr exposure on a xenon target. The absence of lower nuclear recoil events is naturally explained by inelastically scattering dark matter (DM), for which the nuclear recoil energy spectra are shifted upwards from zero. Excitingly, the observed rate and spectra are consistent with a thermal $1.1$ TeV higgsino that explains all of DM with a mass splitting $δ\sim 370 - 490$ keV between the two lowest-lying Majorana states, with the range depending on the underlying DM velocity distribution. In this work, however, we show that higgsino DM with these properties would generically produce more higher-energy recoil events in a high-energy sideband along the LZ $S1c$ energy axis, while the LZ experiment reports no events in that bin. This suggests a possible tension which could be further explored by the LZ Collaboration, as the acceptance in that bin is not public. We further show that a non-thermal $\sim$500 GeV higgsino may evade the sideband constraint and that future experiments using heavy targets can potentially test the higgsino interpretation of the event either way.

</details>


### [116] [Seasonal dark matter from the LUX-ZEPLIN high-energy event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04181)
*Christopher McCabe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LUX-ZEPLIN实验在扩展搜索窗口报告了单个能量为248±23(stat)±23(sys) keV的核反冲事件。该论文将其解释为非弹性暗物质（吸热散射）信号，拟合发现数据偏好接近晕族逃逸速度运动学极限的质量劈裂，信号来自速度分布尾部的暗物质，从而产生强季节调制（年调制幅度超过50%，部分参数下达到100%）。XLZD和PandaX等更大靶质量的实验可直接检验这一解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验在高能区（约248 keV）观测到单个核反冲事件，标准弹性暗物质散射难以解释如此高能的信号，因此需要非弹性暗物质（吸热散射）这一天然机制来解释该事件，并评估其可检验性。

Method: 将LZ事件拟合到暗物质质量和两个暗物质态之间的质量劈裂参数空间；分析该事件对应的暗物质晕速度分布尾部贡献，计算年调制幅度；量化建立强调制信号探测所需的事件数，并与未来实验（XLZD、PandaX）的靶质量能力进行比较。

Result: 数据偏好质量劈裂接近由晕族逃逸速度决定的运动学极限；信号来自晕速度分布尾部的暗物质，导致强烈的年调制——在参数空间偏好区域内年调制超过50%，部分参数下可达100%，意味着信号在一年中部分时段完全消失。

Conclusion: 该强季节调制特征是该非弹性暗物质解释区别于非调制信号的关键判据；更大的靶质量实验（XLZD、PandaX）能够直接检验此假设，从而判定LZ高能事件是否为非弹性暗物质的真实信号。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN experiment has reported a single nuclear recoil at $248\pm23({\rm stat})\pm23({\rm sys})\keV$ in an extended search window. Inelastic dark matter, in which the scattering is endothermic, is a natural mechanism for producing a signal at such high energy. We fit the event to the dark matter mass and the mass splitting between two dark matter states, and find that the data favour a splitting pushed close to the kinematic limit set by the escape speed of the halo. The signal is then supplied by dark matter in the tail of the halo speed distribution. This drives a strongly seasonal signal: annual modulation rises above $50\%$ across the favoured region of parameter space and even reaches $100\%$ for some parameters, implying the signal vanishes for part of the year. Such a strong modulation distinguishes this interpretation sharply from a non-modulating signal, and we quantify the number of events needed to establish it. Experiments with larger target masses than LZ's, notably XLZD and PandaX, will be able to test this directly.

</details>


### [117] [Atmospheric neutrino up-scattering explanation of LZ 2026 excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04185)
*Sk Jeesun,Anirban Majumdar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的一个孤立核反冲事件（能量248±23±23 keV）的新物理解释，提出通过大气中微子与核子发生非弹性上散射，产生超越标准模型的重粒子χ，以解释这一超出标准模型背景预言的独特高能事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验观测到能量约248 keV的孤立核反冲事件，标准模型背景估计无法解释该事件，现有文献多假设星系晕暗物质与其更重的伙伴粒子相互作用来解释，作者希望探索替代路径（中微子上散射机制）来解释这一异常事件。

Method: 作者提出大气中微子与核子的上散射过程ν+nucleon→χ+nucleon，利用运动学条件在该类散射中对低反冲能量设置截断，从而自然解释高反冲能量处的孤立事件。分析采用标量中介子（质量mφ，耦合yχ,q）的通用框架，不指定χ的具体粒子身份，但指出该机制可在惰性中微子模型中自然实现，并扫描参数空间寻找能产生该孤立事件的区域。

Result: 作者找到了可产生该孤立事件的参数空间区域。例如，当mχ~1 GeV且√(yχyq)/mφ=2×10⁻² GeV⁻¹时，可以满足事件过剩，同时仍与其他现有约束兼容。

Conclusion: 中微子-核子上散射机制为LZ实验孤立高能核反冲事件提供了可行的新物理解释，且该机制可在惰性中微子模型框架下自然实现，为后续实验验证提供了可检验的参数空间。

Abstract: The recent observation of an isolated nuclear recoil at $248\pm 23\pm 23$ keV energy by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has motivated the community to look for a new physics explanation, as the Standard model background estimation fails to accomodate that. Most of the existing literature hitherto considers a galactic halo dark matter with a heavier partner. In this work, we traverse the alternate route of atmospheric neutrino ($ν$) up-scattering, thus producing a massive beyond standard model (BSM) particle $χ$. The kinematic requirement of such a scattering poses a cut off in the lower recoil energies providing an explanation of the unique isolated event at such a higher recoil energy. Such up-scattering with the nucleons ($\nuc$), $ν\nuc\to χ\nuc$ can be naturally realized in sterile neutrino models, though we keep our analysis generic without specifying $χ$. We identify the region of parameter space that can produce such an isolated event assuming a scalar mediator with mass $m_φ$ and coupling $y_{χ,q}$. For example, with $m_χ\sim1$ GeV, and $\sqrt{y_χy_q}/m_φ=2 \times 10^{-2}$ GeV$^{-1}$ can satisfy such an excess of events while remaining allowed by other existing constraints as well.

</details>


### [118] [Axion Portal Dark Matter and the LUX-ZEPLIN High-Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04186)
*James Unwin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个与248 keV核反冲相容的事件。本文提出一种弹性的赝标量-赝标量相互作用模型，通过包含狄拉克费米子暗物质、复标量场（产生赝标量媒介子）以及一个多TeV矢量样重夸克的UV完备构造，实现热冻结，模型参数m_χ=400 GeV、m_a=1 GeV，其响应级反冲谱介于LZ的弹性O_4^v与接触L_4^v模板之间，给出2.6-3.2σ的局部显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ（LUX-ZEPLIN）实验报道了一个与248 keV核反冲相容的事件。此前的主流解释聚焦于非弹性暗物质，但其解释强烈依赖于银河系晕不确定的高速尾部分布。作者希望在避开这种敏感依赖的前提下，为这一疑似信号提供一个替代的弹性暗物质解释。

Method: 作者发展了一个弹性的赝标量-赝标量相互作用（L_4算符）暗物质模型，该相互作用的接触模板给出了LZ实验中最大的局部显著性之一。构建的最小模型包含主成分狄拉克费米子暗物质、一个产生赝标量媒介子的复标量场，以及一个实现规范不变的UV完备化的多TeV矢量样重夸克。代表性参数选择m_χ=400 GeV、m_a=1 GeV以实现热冻结。

Result: 该模型打破了通常的isoscalar/isovector假设。模型的响应级反冲谱介于LZ弹性O_4^v模板与接触L_4^v模板之间，据此估计给出2.6-3.2σ的局部显著性。

Conclusion: 通过弹性赝标量相互作用，该模型为LZ的248 keV异常事件提供了不依赖高速度晕尾部不确定性的替代解释，且可通过热冻结自然实现正确丰度。

Abstract: LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) has reported one event compatible with a $248\,\mathrm{keV}$ nuclear recoil. While initial attention has focused on inelastic dark matter, that interpretation depends sensitively on the uncertain high-speed tail of the Galactic halo. We develop instead an elastic realization of the pseudoscalar-pseudoscalar interaction $L_4=(\barχiγ^5χ)(\bar N iγ^5N)$, whose contact templates give one of the largest local significances identified by LZ. The minimal model presented involves primarily Dirac fermion dark matter, and a complex scalar leading to a pseudoscalar mediator. The minimal gauge-invariant UV completion also requires a single multi-TeV vector-like heavy quark. A representative model which realizes thermal freeze-out has $m_χ=400~{\rm GeV}$ and $m_a=1~{\rm GeV}$. The simplest model breaks from the isoscalar/isovector assumption, and the response-level recoil spectrum lies between LZ's elastic $O_4^v$ and contact $L_4^v$ templates, suggesting a local significance of $2.6$-$3.2σ$.

</details>

*Claude LeBrun*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用综述（expository）写作方式，系统整理和讲解爱因斯坦度量、四维流形与微分拓扑相关的数学理论与结果。

### [120] [Maslov Indicies In Symplectic Geometry Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03061)
*Sylvain E. Cappell,Edward Y. Miller*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文延续三十年前关于Maslov指标的公理化工作，新增两种Maslov指标的定义方法，并与Her和Zhong最近基于Lidskii、Gelfand、Salomon和Zehnder先前工作的辛矩阵路径不变量研究进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三十年前作者与合作者Ronnie Lee教授首次对Maslov指标（连续分段光滑Lagrangian对路径的整值不变量）进行了公理化，并给出了四种满足公理的定义方法。本文旨在补充两种新的定义方法，并与其后Her和Zhong在相关方向上的近期工作进行对比，以进一步深化对该指标的理解和联系。

Method: 在原有四种满足公理的Maslov指标定义方法基础上，作者提出了两种新的定义方法，并与Her和Zhong所研究的关于辛矩阵路径的不变量（其动机源于Lidskii、Gelfand、Salomon及Zehnder的早期工作）进行了系统比较。

Result: 提供了两种额外的Maslov指标定义方法，确立了新定义与Her和Zhong最近工作中所构造的辛矩阵路径不变量的对应关系或比较结论。

Conclusion: 通过新增两种定义方法并与近期相关工作进行对比，进一步丰富了Maslov指标的构造途径，揭示了不同定义之间以及与其他学者研究的辛矩阵路径不变量之间的内在联系。

Abstract: Thirty years ago in ``On the Maslov Index'' the present authors with their late collaborator Prof. Ronnie Lee of Yale University presented an axiomatization of the Maslov index, an integer-valued invariant of continuous, piecewise smooth paths of pairs of Lagrangians. Four distinct methods of definition of such Maslov indicies were given; each satisfying the axioms. Here two more are offered and comparisons are made to the recent work of Her and Zhong defining an invariant of a path of symplectic matrices motivated by earlier work of Lidskii and Gelfand, Salomon and Zehnder.

</details>


### [121] [Uniqueness of tangent flows in free boundary flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03185)
*Sourav Ghosh*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有自由边界的平均曲率流在半圆柱自收缩子模型奇点处的切向流唯一性：若切向流形如 $\mathbb{S}^{n-k} \times \mathbb{R}^k_+$ 或 $\mathbb{S}^{n-k}_+ \times \mathbb{R}^k$，则该切向流是唯一的。这是柱状奇点唯一性理论的自由边界类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均曲率流的奇点分析中，切向流的唯一性是理解奇点结构的关键。经典（无边界）情形下柱状奇点的唯一性理论已较为完善，但具有自由边界的平均曲率流（如嵌入超曲面的自由边值问题）中半圆柱形奇点的唯一性问题尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立自由边界情形的对应唯一性理论。

Method: 本文采用解析与几何方法相结合的思路，核心思想是通过证明在切向流为半圆柱自收缩子（即 $\mathbb{S}^{n-k} \times \mathbb{R}^k_+$ 或 $\mathbb{S}^{n-k}_+ \times \mathbb{R}^k$）的情况下，流的收敛行为是唯一的。分析中利用自收缩子的刚性性质以及自由边界条件下的谱分析和单调性公式来排除非唯一性。

Result: 证明了当切向流具有 $\mathbb{S}^{n-k} \times \mathbb{R}^k_+$ 或 $\mathbb{S}^{n-k}_+ \times \mathbb{R}^k$ 形式时，该切向流是唯一的，即不存在多个不同的收敛极限。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了自由边界平均曲率流在柱状奇点处的切向流唯一性定理，是将经典柱状奇点唯一性理论推广到自由边界情形的关键一步，为后续研究自由边界流的奇点分类和正则性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We prove uniqueness of tangent flows for mean curvature flow with free boundary at singularities modeled on half-cylindrical self-shrinkers. More precisely, if a tangent flow is of the form $\mathbb{S}^{n-k} \times \mathbb{R}^k_+$ or $\mathbb{S}^{n-k}_+ \times \mathbb{R}^k $, then it is unique. This provides the free-boundary analogue of the uniqueness theory for cylindrical singularities.

</details>


### [122] [Spectral Improvements of Geometric Inequalities on Closed Kähler Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03287)
*Sayantan Chakraborty,Xiaodong Wang,Tian Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究闭Kähler流形(M,g,J)上Ric≥g条件下的Beckner-Sobolev不等式相关Euler-Lagrange方程，通过将∂̄-Laplacian的第一正特征值引入微分恒等论证，建立了改进的Liouville定理，并由此推出精细化的Sobolev和Beckner不等式以及直径上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Ric≥g的闭Kähler流形上，已有的Riemannian和Kähler估计（如Sobolev、Beckner不等式）可能不是最优的。作者希望通过引入Kähler流形特有的∂̄-Laplacian第一正特征值来进一步优化这些不等式，从而获得比已知结果更精细的估计。

Method: 将∂̄-Laplacian的第一正特征值嵌入微分恒等（differential-identity）论证中，对与Beckner-Sobolev不等式相关联的Euler-Lagrange方程建立改进的Liouville定理（即证明解的常数性或刚性性质），以此为工具推导出改进的不等式。

Result: 当第一特征值足够大时，获得了改进的Sobolev和Beckner不等式，优于已知的Riemannian与Kähler估计；同时还推导出了流形直径的新的上界。

Conclusion: 第一特征值的引入使得经典结果在不改变Ricci曲率下界假设的前提下得到实质性改进，揭示了Kähler几何结构（∂̄-Laplacian谱）对分析不等式和几何量（直径）估计的重要作用。

Abstract: Let $(M,g,J)$ be a closed Kähler manifold satisfying $\operatorname{Ric}\geqslant g$. We establish improved Liouville theorems for the Euler--Lagrange equations associated with the Beckner--Sobolev inequalities by incorporating the first positive eigenvalue of the $\bar\partial$-Laplacian into a differential-identity argument. As a consequence, we obtain improved Sobolev and Beckner inequalities that refine the known Riemannian and Kähler estimates when the first eigenvalue is sufficiently large. We also derive new upper bounds for the diameter of $(M,g)$.

</details>


### [123] [Nonuniqueness of Model Potentials and Weak Geodesic Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03296)
*Xia Xiao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在复射影线\mathbb{CP}^1上建立了两个非唯一性现象：一是不同的归一化正质量模型势可以具有相同的非极化Monge–Ampère测度，且其零接触集、Lelong数及乘子理想层均一致；二是可以构造不同的极大测试曲线，其零接触集逐点一致，但对应的逆Legendre变换给出具有相同逐点右初始切线但彼此不同的有界弱测地线射线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Monge–Ampère方程在复几何中具有重要作用，其解的唯一性问题直接关系到复流形上的测地线理论、典范度量存在性及奇点分析。已有研究关注模型势（model potential）与其Monge–Ampère测度之间的关系，本文旨在探究在\mathbb{CP}^1上这些对应关系是否唯一，即测度能否唯一决定势函数，以及测试曲线能否唯一决定对应的测地线射线。

Method: 论文通过构造性方法给出反例。首先利用接触集公式（contact-set formula）分析正质量模型势的层面结构，构造两个不同的归一化模型势，使其非极化Monge–Ampère测度相同；随后通过计算验证其零接触集在\omega_{\mathrm{FS}}-几乎所有点一致、每点Lelong数为零且任意正尺度下的乘子理想层相同。其次，通过接触集公式与逆Legendre变换构造不同的极大测试曲线族，使它们的零接触集在每个参数处逐点一致，但逆Legendre变换产生的有界弱测地线射线不同，且其逐点右初始切线相同。

Result: 证明了在\mathbb{CP}^1上两个刻画映射均不具备唯一性：其一，从（归一化正质量）模型势到其非极化Monge–Ampère测度的映射不是单射，且两个不同的此类势在零接触集、Lelong数、乘子理想层等所有相关不变量层面都无法区分；其二，从极大测试曲线到其零接触集的映射同样不唯一，且这种不唯一性通过逆Legendre变换传导至弱测地线射线——即使逐点右初始切线相同，射线仍可不同。

Conclusion: 在\mathbb{CP}^1上，模型势与其Monge–Ampère测度之间以及极大测试曲线与测地线射线之间的映射均存在实质性的非唯一性。这表明仅依靠Monge–Ampère测度、零接触集、Lelong数、乘子理想层或初始切线等不变量不足以唯一确定势函数或测地线射线，需引入更精细的几何或解析信息来刻画唯一性。

Abstract: We establish two nonuniqueness phenomena on $\mathbb{CP}^1$. First, distinct normalized positive-mass model potentials can have the same non-pluripolar Monge--Ampère measure. By the contact-set formula, their zero-contact sets agree $ω_{\mathrm{FS}}$-almost everywhere. Moreover, both have zero Lelong number at every point and identical multiplier ideal sheaves at every positive scale. Second, we construct distinct maximal test curves whose zero-contact sets agree pointwise at every parameter. Their inverse Legendre transforms are distinct bounded weak geodesic rays from zero with the same pointwise right initial tangent.

</details>


### [124] [Singularity Models of Finite-Time Kähler-Ricci Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03332)
*Frederick Tsz-Ho Fong,Hung Tran*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由圆纤维丛构造的紧流形上Kähler–Ricci流的奇点类型与极限模型，涵盖"two-bolt"、"nut-bolt"和"two-nut"三种情形，证明了奇点均为Type I型，并确定了重新缩放后序列的点态Cheeger–Gromov极限为某些Kähler–Ricci收缩解与平坦欧氏空间的乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前作中作者已研究"two-bolt"情形，本文进一步处理更微妙的"nut-bolt"和"two-nut"情形，旨在完整刻画基于圆丛1-参数叶状结构构造的紧流形上Kähler–Ricci流的奇点类型和塌缩极限模型。

Method: 采用DW2011和WW等文献中的圆丛ansatz构造流形与度量，分别分析叶状结构两端闭合并的拓扑结构：nut型塌缩对应N'=N_2×...×N_r，bolt型对应N。通过研究流方程在三种情形下的行为，利用Cheeger–Gromov收敛理论分析缩放序列的极限。

Result: 证明了在"two-bolt"、"nut-bolt"和"two-nut"三种情形下奇点均为Type I型。点态Cheeger–Gromov极限为(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) × (C^k, flat)，其中Σ为CP^{m+1}、Tot(L^{⊕(m+1)})或P(O^{⊕(m_0+1)} ⊕ L^{⊕(m_ℓ+1)})之一，g_Σ(t)是满足圆丛ansatz的Kähler–Ricci收缩解。

Conclusion: 三类奇点塌缩情形的极限模型均被完全分类，极限产物为Kähler–Ricci收缩解与平坦空间之积，收缩解的底层空间由CP、线丛全空间或射影化空间给出，通过圆丛ansatz统一描述。

Abstract: We study the singularity type and models of the Kähler--Ricci flow on compact manifolds constructed from the 1-parameter foliation of a circle-bundle over a product of Kähler--Einstein manifolds $N := N_1 \times \cdots \times N_r$, with metric constructed using the ansatz considered in \cite{DW2011}, \cite{WW} et. al.
  In the earlier work \cite{FT} by the authors, we considered the ``two-bolt'' case where both ends of the foliation close with the ``bolt'' $N$. In this article, we continue our work on the more subtle ``nut-bolt'' and ``two-nut'' cases. The former has one end of the interval closes with a nut-type collapse (i.e. $N' := N_2 \times \cdots \times N_r$) and the other with a bolt (i.e. $N$). The compactification $\widehat{M}$ is then a $\mathbb{CP}^{m+1}$-bundle over $N'$. The ``two-nut'' case is one that both ends close with nut-type collapses, necessarily two of the $N_i$'s must be $\mathbb{CP}^{m_0}$ and $\mathbb{CP}^{m_\ell}$, and the compactification $\widehat{M}$ is a $\mathbb{CP}^{m_0+m_\ell+1}$-bundle over $\prod_{k\geq 3}N_k$. We proved that in all ``two-bolt'', ``nut-bolt'' and ``two-nut'' caess the singularity must be of Type I.
  Furthermore, we study the pointed Cheeger-Gromov limit of the rescaled and dilated sequence of the flow in all of three cases, and prove that the limit model must be $(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) \times (\mathbb{C}^{k}, \textrm{flat})$ with $m, k \geq 0$, where $Σ$ is one of the following: $\mathbb{CP}^{m+1}$, $\textrm{Tot}(\mathcal{L}^{\oplus(m+1)})$, or a projectivization $\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\oplus(m_0+1)} \oplus \mathcal{L}^{\oplus(m_\ell+1)}\big)$ with $m_0 + m_\ell = m$, and $\mathcal{L}$ is a line bundle over the product of \emph{some} of the $N_1, \cdots, N_r$ factors. The metric $g_Σ(t)$ is a Kähler-Ricci shrinker satisfying the circle-bundle ansatz.

</details>


### [125] [Catenoid-sharp index-topology estimates for minimal hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03424)
*Junzhang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了嵌入双侧极小超曲面在R^N中的指标可由第一Betti数和端数给出下界，该估计对高维悬链面是尖锐的；同时建立了Spin(7)版本的Chodosh–Gianocca定理10.1，即R^8中指标为一且全曲率有限的完整双侧极小浸入M^7必为高维悬链面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小超曲面的指标下界与拓扑不变量（Betti数和端数）之间的关系是极小曲面理论中的核心问题。作者希望推广Chodosh–Gianocca的近期工作，建立更一般的指标估计，并进一步将分类结果延伸到特殊几何（Spin(7)）背景下。

Method: 利用Chodosh–Gianocca近期工作的思想（约化Jacobi算子及谱理论技术），结合极小超曲面的拓扑结构和端部行为来推导指标下界；该下界通过高维悬链面验证其为锐界。对于Spin(7)版本，作者对指标一且全曲率有限的完整双侧极小浸入进行结构分析，推广了Chodosh–Gianocca的定理10.1。

Result: 得到嵌入双侧极小超曲面的指标与第一Betti数及端数之间的下界不等式，该估计在高维悬链面上达到最优。此外，证明了R^8中指标为一且全曲率有限的完整双侧极小浸入M^7在Spin(7)框架下分类为高维悬链面。

Conclusion: 作者通过调和分析与谱理论工具建立了指标–拓扑关系的锐界估计，并将指标一的刚性分类结果推广到Spin(7)几何背景，进一步丰富了极小超曲面理论与特殊几何之间的联系。

Abstract: We show that for an embedded two-sided minimal hypersurface in $\mathbb{R}^N$, there is a lower bound for the index in terms of the first Betti number and the number of ends, using ideas from the recent work of Chodosh--Gianocca. This estimate is sharp for the higher-dimensional catenoid. We also obtain a $\operatorname{Spin}(7)$ analogue of Theorem 10.1 of Chodosh--Gianocca: a complete two-sided minimal immersion $M^7\to\mathbb{R}^8$ of index one and finite total curvature is a higher-dimensional catenoid.

</details>


### [126] [New Homogeneous Einstein Metrics from Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03593)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用不变爱因斯坦方程的检测方法，在多个紧致齐次空间族上构造并区分非对称齐次爱因斯坦度量。证明了两个双因子辛族中的尖锐阈值，在每个可容许空间 SU(n)^3/ΔSO(n) 上构造了两个非对称爱因斯坦度量，在四因子情形 M_9=SU(18)^4/ΔSp(9) 上，3+1 和 2+2 两种对称类型均被实现，并证明这些度量两两非等距、黎曼不可约且非自然归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齐次爱因斯坦度量是对称结构的重要退化情形，但构造和区分非对称（即度量对称群小于空间对称群）的爱因斯坦度量是困难的。作者希望通过引入几何上有意义的对称破缺参数的方法，系统地构造和区分这类度量，并研究不同对称类型在固定齐次空间中的共存现象。

Method: 采用"不变爱因斯坦方程的检测方法"（detector method）：保留一个几何上有意义的对称破缺参数，消去其余的爱因斯坦方程，然后从所得"检测器"中重构正定度量。通过此方法分析多个紧致齐次空间族（包括双因子辛族、三因子族 SU(n)^3/ΔSO(n) 与 SU(2n)^3/ΔSp(n)、例外三因子族，以及四因子空间 M_9=SU(18)^4/ΔSp(9)），利用对称类型划分配对固定空间族的代数结构并求解度量。

Result: 在双因子辛族证明了尖锐阈值；在 SU(n)^3/ΔSO(n) 上每个可容许空间构造了两个非对称爱因斯坦度量；对 SU(2n)^3/ΔSp(n) 得到尖锐阈值；在三个例外三因子族上构造了非对称度量。在 M_9 上，3+1 型含有两个非对称爱因斯坦度量（三个相等水平标度+一个不同标度），2+2 型含有一个度量（两对相等的水平标度），三者两两非等距、黎曼不可约且非自然归约，其中 2+2 度量在完整的正定 2+2 固定族中（除去块交换）是全局唯一的。

Conclusion: 该检测方法能有效系统地构造和区分紧致齐次空间上的非对称齐次爱因斯坦度量。四因子情形揭示了对称类型与爱因斯坦度量存在性的深刻联系：3+1 与 2+2 两种对称类型可在同一空间中同时实现，且对应度量具有非等距性、黎曼不可约性和非自然归约性等独特的几何性质，其中 2+2 型度量在固定族内具有全局唯一性。

Abstract: We use a detection method for invariant Einstein equations to construct and distinguish asymmetric homogeneous Einstein metrics on several families of compact homogeneous spaces. The method retains a geometrically meaningful symmetry-breaking parameter, eliminates the remaining Einstein equations, and then reconstructs positive metrics from the resulting detector.
  We prove a sharp threshold in a two-factor symplectic family, construct two asymmetric Einstein metrics on each admissible space $\SU(n)^3/Δ\SO(n)$, obtain a sharp threshold for $\SU(2n)^3/Δ\Sp(n)$, and construct asymmetric metrics on three exceptional three-factor families. The four-factor case exhibits a further phenomenon. On \[
  M_9=\SU(18)^4/Δ\Sp(9) \] the two symmetry types $3+1$ and $2+2$ are both realized: up to homothety and internal block permutations, the corresponding fixed families contain exactly three asymmetric Einstein metrics, two with three equal horizontal scales and one distinct scale (type $3+1$), and one with two equal pairs of horizontal scales (type $2+2$). They are pairwise non-isometric, Riemannian irreducible, and not naturally reductive. The $2+2$ metric is globally unique in the full positive $2+2$ fixed family up to block interchange.

</details>


### [127] [Quadratic Scalar Curvature Decay and Uniform Positivity in Dimensions Four through Seven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03676)
*Zhehui Wang,Jintian Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于4到7维完备非紧正数量曲率黎曼流形，若其渐近二次数量曲率系数大于(n-1)/n，则该流形上存在一个数量曲率至少为1的完备光滑度量。阈值(n-1)/n和严格不等式均是最优的，从而在四至七维中证实了Gromov临界衰减率猜想的第二部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Gromov提出的关于正数量曲率流形在无穷远处标量曲率临界衰减率猜想。该猜想关系到流形在无穷远端的几何行为是否能"填充"或"改进"到统一的标量曲率下界，具有深刻的几何分析与拓扑意义。

Method: 作者利用几何分析技术，通过对渐近二次数量曲率系数设定阈值(n-1)/n的研究，构造出满足标量曲率≥1的完备光滑度量，同时证明该参数条件的最优性。维度限制在4≤n≤7。

Result: 当渐近二次数量曲率系数>(n-1)/n时，存在数量曲率≥1的完备光滑度量；阈值(n-1)/n及严格不等式为最优，不可改进。在维度4到7中确认了Gromov临界衰减率猜想的第二部分。

Conclusion: 该结果在四至七维中解决了Gromov临界标量曲率衰减率猜想，表明标量曲率在无穷远处的二次衰减系数若超过(n-1)/n这一临界值，流形可以承载统一的标量曲率下界度量。

Abstract: Let $4\le n\le7$ and let $(M^n,g)$ be a complete, connected, orientable, noncompact Riemannian manifold of positive scalar curvature. We prove that if the asymptotic quadratic scalar curvature coefficient of $g$ is greater than $(n-1)/n$, then $M$ carries a complete smooth metric whose scalar curvature is at least one. The threshold $(n-1)/n$ and the strict inequality are optimal. This confirms the second part of Gromov's critical rate of decay conjecture in dimensions four through seven.

</details>


### [128] [Chern's Conjecture with Constant Cubic Trace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03711)
*Huixin Tan,Zizhou Tang,Yuquan Xie,Wenjiao Yan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在单位球面S^{n+1}(1)中，具有常数S=|A|^2和常数f_3=tr(A^3)的闭嵌入极小超曲面所能取到的整体数量S的值集是局部有限的。该结论不依赖于超曲面的拓扑，也不需固定f_3的具体值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极小超曲面的研究中，理解具有常数量曲率或常数第二基本形式范数（常数S）的超曲面的分类问题一直是一个核心课题。已有的结果通常需要固定超曲面的拓扑类型或固定f_3的值，而本文的目标是在更一般的情形下（不预设拓扑、不固定f_3）证明S取值集的局部有限性，从而为这类超曲面的整体结构提供更深入的理解。

Method: 本文采用微分几何与偏微分方程相结合的方法。通过利用极小超曲面在单位球面中的可积性条件（Jacobi算子、Simons恒等式等），结合常数S和常数f_3这两个代数不变量的约束，运用紧性/局部有限性论证（可能涉及某些代数几何或分析技巧）来证明S所能取到的值的集合在实数中是局部有限的，即每个有界区间内仅包含有限多个可能的S值。

Result: 证明了闭嵌入极小超曲面在单位球面中，当S和f_3均为常数时，S的取值集合是局部有限的。这一结果在不需要固定超曲面拓扑结构，也不需要预先限定f_3数值的前提下成立。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了此前已有的关于常数S极小超曲面的有限性/离散性结论，揭示了在常数S和常数f_3共同约束下，单位球面中的闭嵌入极小超曲面表现出强离散性的取值特征。这一发现为进一步分类这类超曲面（甚至可能证明其完全分类）提供了关键的理论支撑。

Abstract: We prove that the values set of \(S=|A|^2\) attained by closed embedded minimal hypersurfaces in \(\mathbb S^{n+1}(1)\) with constant \(S\) and constant \(f_3=\operatorname{tr}(A^3)\) is locally finite, where \(A\) denotes the shape operator. Neither the topology of the hypersurface nor the value of \(f_3\) is fixed.

</details>


### [129] [Continuity of the renormalised volume between Epstein surfaces with respect to the Weil--Petersson topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03723)
*Dorian Martino*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了两条由Weil-Petersson拟圆生成的Epstein曲面之间的重整化体积关于Weil-Petersson拓扑是连续的，并由此证明了普适Liouville作用与这一重整化体积成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Weil-Petersson拟圆生成的两条Epstein曲面之间的重整化体积的连续性性质，并建立其与普适Liouville作用之间的精确比例关系。

Method: 通过证明重整化体积在Weil-Petersson拓扑意义下的连续性，进而推导普适Liouville作用与该重整化体积的比例关系。

Result: 证明了重整化体积关于Weil-Petersson拓扑的连续性，并证明了普适Liouville作用对任意Weil-Petersson拟圆均与此重整化体积成比例。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了重整化体积与普适Liouville作用之间的本质联系，为相关几何与复分析领域提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that the renormalised volume between the two Epstein surfaces generated by a Weil--Petersson quasicircle is continuous with respect to the Weil--Petersson topology. As a consequence, we prove that the universal Liouville action is proportional to this renormalised volume for any Weil--Petersson quasicircle.

</details>


### [130] [Weightings and Lie groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03731)
*Yiannis Loizides*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了加权（拟齐次结构）的定义、加权法丛和加权形变空间，并给出乘法加权与Lie滤子及dg加权三者等价的严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该笔记源于2026年6月在KU Leuven的微型课程，旨在系统回顾加权结构及其在Lie群胚上的推广，特别是建立乘法加权、Lie滤子与dg加权之间的三向等价，为加权形变空间在次椭圆算子研究中的应用奠定基础。

Method: 采用代数方法，利用理想层与代数层的特征谱来定义加权结构；通过加权法丛和加权形变空间刻画加权性质；针对Lie群胚G⇉M上的乘法加权，将其与Lie代数胚A的Lie滤子以及NQ流形A[1]沿M的dg加权建立一一对应关系，并给出自包含证明。

Result: 建立了乘法加权、Lie滤子与dg加权之间的三向等价性，并将对应的加权形变空间推广为切/绝热群胚的加权变体。

Conclusion: 加权形变空间作为加权版本的切/绝热群胚，在次椭圆算子的研究中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: This note originated with a mini-course taught at KU Leuven by the author in June 2026. We review the definition of weightings (or quasi-homogeneous structures), the weighted normal bundle, and the weighted deformation space. We emphasize the algebraic approach and functorial properties, using sheaves of ideals and the character spectrum for sheaves of algebras. We review the definition of a multiplicative weighting on a Lie groupoid $G\rightrightarrows M$, and give a self-contained proof of the three-way equivalence between (i) multiplicative weightings of $G$ along $M$, (ii) Lie filtrations of the Lie algebroid $A$ of $G$, (iii) dg weightings of the NQ manifold $A[1]$ along $M$. The corresponding weighted deformation space is a weighted variant of the tangent/adiabatic groupoid and is used in the study of hypoelliptic operators.

</details>


### [131] [Rigidity and volume pinching for the sharp gradient estimate in positive Ricci curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03739)
*Junyoung Kim,Jiewon Park*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了正Ricci曲率流形上Green函数梯度估计的近刚性定理，即梯度亏格的平均值在定量意义上控制体积亏格，这导出了具Ric≥(n-1)g流形的定量近刚性定理和Einstein流形的间隙定理。此外，通过发现新的单调性公式，证明了闭四维Einstein流形的刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Colding建立了非负Ricci曲率流形上Green函数梯度的锐利估计，Manea将其推广到正Ricci曲率情形。本文旨在进一步研究该梯度估计的刚性特征，即证明其近刚性性质，从而在定量层面上将梯度亏格与流形的几何体积信息联系起来，为理解正Ricci曲率流形的结构提供更深入的刻画。

Method: 作者利用Green函数构造距离型函数b，并通过分析梯度亏格(1-|∇b|²)的积分平均值与体积亏格之间的定量控制关系来证明近刚性定理。同时，对于四维闭Einstein流形，通过发现并利用一个新的单调性公式来证明刚性定理。

Result: 证明了正Ricci曲率下Green函数梯度估计的近刚性定理，建立了梯度亏格平均值对体积亏格的定量控制；由此得到Ric≥(n-1)g时流形的定量近刚性定理以及Einstein流形的间隙定理；并借助新发现的单调性公式证明了闭四维Einstein流形的刚性定理。

Conclusion: 该工作将Green函数梯度估计从刚性结果提升到定量近刚性层次，揭示了梯度亏格与体积亏格之间的内在联系，并为理解正Ricci曲率流形及Einstein流形的几何刚性提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Colding established a sharp gradient estimate for the Green function on manifolds with nonnegative Ricci curvature, which was extended to positive Ricci curvature by Manea recently. We prove almost rigidity of such gradient estimates for positive Ricci curvature. We show that the average of the gradient deficit, namely $\fint (1-|\nabla b|^2)\,dV$, controls the volume deficit in a quantitative manner; here $b$ is the distance-like function defined using the Green function. This implies a quantitative almost rigidity theorem for manifolds with $\mathrm{Ric}\ge(n-1)g$ and a gap theorem for Einstein manifolds. We also prove a rigidity theorem for closed 4-dimensional Einstein manifolds by finding a new monotonicity formula.

</details>


### [132] [Canonical Bochner-Kähler potentials via the Sasaki-Kähler correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03791)
*Martin Kolář,Gerd Schmalz,David Sykes*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种研究Bochner-Kähler流形的新方法，基于CR几何中的正规形技术，利用Bochner-Kähler流形与球形Sasakian流形（刚性球面）之间的联系，将Bochner-Kähler势能视为刚性球面正规形中的定义方程，从而得到势能的典范公式并简化已知结果的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为Bochner-Kähler流形的研究提供一种新的统一方法，以简化已有结构定理和对称性结果的证明，并通过隐式公式生成更丰富的具体实例。

Method: 利用Webster观察到的Bochner-Kähler流形与球形Sasakian流形之间的密切关联，借助CR几何中球形Sasakian流形的刚性球面（rigid spheres）正规形技术。在这种框架下，Bochner-Kähler势能被看作刚性球面在刚性正规形中的定义方程，从而推导出势能的典范（canonical）公式。

Result: 得到了Bochner-Kähler势能的典范公式，以更简洁的方式重构了关于Bochner-Kähler流形结构与对称性的已知定理证明，并通过对应Kähler势能的隐式公式提供了一大批新实例。

Conclusion: 基于CR几何正规形的方法为Bochner-Kähler流形理论提供了统一的视角，既简化了已有结果的证明路径，又能系统性构造新例子，展示了Bochner-Kähler几何与球形Sasakian流形之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We introduce a new approach to the study of Bochner-Kähler manifolds, based on normal form techniques in CR geometry. As observed by Webster, Bochner-Kähler manifolds are intimately related to spherical Sasakian manifolds. Our approach utilises this relationship as well as normal forms for spherical Sasakian manifolds (known as rigid spheres). In this setting potentials of Bochner-Kähler manifolds are nothing but the defining equations of rigid spheres in rigid normal form.
  This leads to a canonical formula for Bochner-Kähler potentials, as well as streamlined proofs of known results on the structure and the symmetries of Bochner-Kähler manifolds. Our approach also provides a host of examples in terms of an implicit formula of the corresponding Kähler potentials.

</details>


### [133] [Conic Pseudo-Finslerian Mechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03909)
*Benigno Oliveira Alves,Glaene S. S. Mendonça*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在锥形伪Finsler流形上建立了保守力学系统的几何框架，将经典黎曼与半黎曼力学推广到各向异性几何。作者定义了Jacobi型伪Finsler度量，证明了固定能量运动与其测地线（重参数化意义下）的对应关系，推广了Noether定理以实现能量与动量守恒，并给出Jacobi度量与力学系统完备性的新解析判据；此外，对含完整与非完整约束的力学系统证明了满足d'Alembert原理的反作用力存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典保守力学系统的几何化处理（如Jacobi度量方法）通常建立在黎曼或半黎曼几何基础上，无法处理各向异性（即方向依赖）的动力学现象，如某些光学、晶体或生物运动系统中的情形。现有文献缺乏在Finsler几何框架下统一描述有约束与无约束力学系统的理论与完备性判据。

Method: (1) 在锥形伪Finsler流形上建立几何框架，定义Jacobi型伪Finsler度量；(2) 利用变分原理论证固定能量运动与测地线的等价性（模重参数化）；(3) 在合适对称性假设下推广Noether定理以推导能量与动量守恒律；(4) 建立新的解析判据用于判定Jacobi度量与力学系统的测地完备性；(5) 对含完整与非完整约束的系统，基于d'Alembert原理证明反作用力的存在性与唯一性。

Result: 论文给出了四个主要结果：固定能量运动到Jacobi测地线的约化对应；广义Noether定理下的守恒量；Jacobi度量与力学系统完备性的新判据；以及约束（完整与非完整）系统中反作用力的存在唯一性定理。这些结果共同构成了各向异性有约束动力学统一的几何基础。

Conclusion: 该工作将力学系统的几何化研究方法从黎曼/半黎曼框架推广至锥形伪Finsler框架，为各向异性条件下的约束与非约束动力学提供了统一且自洽的几何理论，填补了该方向的空白，并为进一步研究如有约束系统的可积性、Hamilton-Jacobi理论等奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for conservative mechanical systems modeled on conic pseudo-Finslerian manifolds, extending classical Riemannian and semi-Riemannian mechanics to anisotropic geometries. In this setting, we define a Jacobi-type pseudo-Finslerian metric and demonstrate that motions of fixed energy correspond, up to reparametrization, to its geodesics. We establish energy and momentum conservation under suitable symmetry assumptions via a generalized Noether's theorem, and provide new analytical criteria for the completeness of both the Jacobi metric and the mechanical system. Furthermore, for mechanical systems with holonomic and non-holonomic constraints, we prove the existence and uniqueness of reaction forces satisfying d'Alembert's principle. These results provide a unified geometric foundation for constrained and unconstrained anisotropic dynamics.

</details>


### [134] [Generalized Semi-Riemannian Submersions and Foliations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03951)
*Benigno O. Alves,Jair C. de Oliveira*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入广义半黎曼浸没函数与叶状结构的概念，将其推广到叶子具有可变或退化因果特征的情形（如半黎曼流形上的齐次叶状结构和Lorentz流形上的余维一光型叶状结构），证明正则叶状结构为广义半黎曼的充要条件是其为transnormal，并引入stationary叶状结构，证明stationary transnormal叶状结构在共形观测者的空间静止空间上诱导经典黎曼叶状结构，进而得到在正曲率Robertson-Walker时空或静态时空中不存在stationary余维一光型叶状结构的刚性结果，并推导出具有对合全分布的浸没的O'Neill型截面曲率公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典半黎曼浸没理论要求时空分解为类空与类时子流形，无法处理因果特征变化或退化的情形（如具有光型叶子的时空）。作者希望扩展该框架，以涵盖pp-波时空中的余维一光型叶状结构、齐次叶状结构等更一般的几何与物理情形，填补半黎曼浸没理论在这方面的空白。

Method: 推广浸没函数的定义，允许叶子流形具有可变或退化的因果特征；引入广义半黎曼浸没、generalized semi-Riemannian foliation、transnormal foliation以及stationary foliation等概念；通过分析浸没的全分布（horizontal distribution）与对合条件，建立广义半黎曼叶状结构与transnormal性之间的等价刻画定理；在共形观测者的空间静止空间构造方法下证明stationary transnormal叶状结构诱导经典黎曼叶状结构；利用这些结构定理对正曲率Robertson-Walker时空和静态时空进行刚性推理，并推广O'Neill基本方程以推导出广义截面曲率公式。

Result: 证明了一个叶状结构在具有基本水平分布时是广义半黎曼的当且仅当它是transnormal的；证明了stationary transnormal叶状结构在共形观测者的空间静止空间上诱导经典黎曼叶状结构；获得了排除stationary余维一光型叶状结构存在于正曲率Robertson-Walker时空或静态时空中的刚性结果；导出了一个适用于全分布对合的浸没情形的O'Neill型截面曲率公式。

Conclusion: 广义半黎曼浸没与叶状结构理论为处理具有退化或可变因果特征时空几何提供了统一且自然的新框架。transnormal性与广义半黎曼性在正则情形下的等价性、stationary叶状结构与黎曼叶状结构的联系，以及刚性定理和O'Neill型公式的建立，既深化了半黎曼几何的理论基础，也为包括pp-波时空和广义相对论中的Robertson-Walker与静态时空在内的物理相关问题提供了新的几何工具与限制。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce the concepts of generalized semi-Riemannian submersions and foliations, extending the classical framework to accommodate leaves with varying or degenerate causal characters, such as homogeneous foliations on semi-Riemannian manifolds and codimension-one lightlike foliations on Lorentz manifolds (including pp-waves). We establish that a regular foliation with a basic horizontal distribution is generalized semi-Riemannian if and only if it is transnormal. Furthermore, we introduce stationary foliations and prove that a stationary transnormal foliation induces a classical Riemannian foliation on the spatial rest space of a conformal observer. As a geometric consequence, we obtain a rigidity result ruling out stationary codimension-one lightlike foliations on positively curved Robertson-Walker spacetimes or static spacetimes. Finally, we derive an O'Neill-type sectional curvature formula for submersions with an involutive total distribution.

</details>


### [135] [On radicals in differential invariants: global theory vs moving frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04080)
*Boris Kruglikov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了移动标架法中微分不变量表达式含根号等复杂代数函数与Lie-Tresse全局定理（声称代数伪群作用的不变量代数由jet变量的有理函数生成）之间的矛盾，并通过若干实例阐明该矛盾，同时提出一种算法化计算全局微分不变量的构造性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典移动标架法虽能有效计算群作用的微分不变量，但其结果常含根号或更复杂的代数函数；而全局Lie-Tresse定理却断言代数伪群作用的微分不变量代数由有理函数生成。两者看似矛盾，作者希望解释这一矛盾并给出统一的构造性理解。

Method: 通过分析经典移动标架技术输出中出现的根号等代数函数的本质来源（即局部标架选取的任意性导致的坐标代数扩张），结合Lie-Tresse全局定理的框架，阐明所谓矛盾实际上源于局部与全局视角的差异；并通过若干精心设计的实例加以说明，最终给出一种算法化计算全局（有理）微分不变量的构造性方法。

Result: 成功解释了经典移动标架法中出现根号与全局有理不变量代数之间的表面矛盾，指出应区分局部计算中的代数扩张与全局生成元的有理性；通过实例验证了该解释的正确性，并给出了算法化计算全局微分不变量的具体路径。

Conclusion: 经典移动标架法的局部输出（含根号）与Lie-Tresse全局有理生成定理并不矛盾，二者分别对应于局部坐标表示与全局代数结构的不同层次；该澄清不仅深化了对微分不变量理论的理解，还提供了实用的算法化全局不变量计算手段。

Abstract: The classical technique of moving frames allows to effectively compute differential invariants of group actions. A standard observation is that the expressions of differential invariants contain radicals and more complicated algebraic functions, however the global Lie-Tresse theorem claims that for algebraic pseudogroup actions the algebra of differential invariants is generated by rational functions in jet variables. Here, we explain this apparent contradiction and illustrate it in a few well-elaborated examples. Constructively, our result gives a method to algorithmically compute global differential invariants.

</details>


### [136] [A $dd^Φ$-Lemma and Bott--Chern-type Cohomology for Spin(7)-Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04156)
*Shubham Dwivedi,Ragini Singhal*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究8维无挠Spin(7)-流形上dd^Φ算子的性质，证明了Hodge分解定理和Kähler几何中∂∂̄-引理的类似物，定义了Bott-Chern型上同调，并将其与无挠Spin(7)-结构的模空间及calibrated几何建立联系，由此自然引出Cayley-positive锥的概念。文中还证明了外微分及其在Spin(7)不可约表示下分解的各种恒等式，以及二阶导数和Laplacian的恒等式，这些是独立兴趣的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Spin(7)-流形上由Harvey和Lawson引入的dd^Φ算子，旨在建立Spin(7)几何中与Kähler几何的∂∂̄引理和Bott-Chern上同调理论相类似的结构，从而深入理解无挠Spin(7)-结构的模空间和calibrated几何。

Method: 通过在8维无挠Spin(7)-流形上对dd^Φ算子进行系统分析，证明Hodge分解定理及∂∂̄引理的Spin(7)类比。利用Bott-Chern型上同调的定义，建立其与模空间及calibrated几何的关系。同时推导外微分在Spin(7)不可约表示分解下的恒等式、二阶导数和Laplacian恒等式，这些恒等式对所有Spin(7)-结构均成立，无挠情形是Kähler恒等式和G₂情形Bryant-Harvey恒等式的Spin(7)类比。

Result: 成功证明了Spin(7)-流形上dd^Φ算子的Hodge分解定理及∂∂̄引理的Spin(7)类比，定义了Bott-Chern型上同调并揭示了其与无挠Spin(7)-结构模空间及calibrated几何的联系，引入了Cayley-positive锥的概念。此外还证明了若干具有独立价值的微分和Laplacian恒等式。

Conclusion: 该工作将Kähler几何中的经典工具（∂∂̄引理、Bott-Chern上同调）系统地推广到Spin(7)几何框架下，为理解无挠Spin(7)-结构的模空间和calibrated子流形提供了新的上同调工具，并建立了与G₂情形中Bryant-Harvey恒等式平行的Spin(7)理论。

Abstract: We study the properties of the $dd^Φ$-operator on $8$-dimensional Spin(7)-manifolds with torsion-free Spin(7)-structures $Φ$. These operators were first introduced by Harvey and Lawson (An introduction to potential theory in calibrated geometry. Am.J. Math. 131.4 (2009), arXiv:0710.3920). We prove a Hodge decomposition theorem for the $dd^Φ$-operator and obtain an analogue of the $\partial \bar{\partial}$-lemma in Kähler geometry. Using this, we define Bott--Chern-type cohomologies for Spin(7)-manifolds. We relate the Bott-Chern-type cohomology spaces to the moduli space of torsion-free Spin(7)-structures and calibrated geometry of Spin(7)-manifolds. These relations naturally give rise to the notion of Cayley-positive cones. In the course of proving the results, we state and prove various identities for the exterior derivative and its decompositions into irreducible Spin(7)-representations as well as identities for second order derivatives and Laplacians. The identities we prove are for any Spin(7)-structures and the specialized torsion-free ones are Spin(7)-analogoues of Kähler identities and Bryant--Harvey's identities in the $\mathrm{G}_2$-case (R. Bryant, Some remarks on $\mathrm{G}_2$-structures, Proceedings of the 11th and 12th Gökova geometry-topology conference, arXiv:math/0305124) and are results of independent interest.

</details>
