We also derive closed finite-volume sources for the RGZ stationary equations.
  The massive response is exponentially suppressed at large volume, whereas the massless factor depends on the global zero-mode normalization. Thus the Gaussian calculation does not by itself establish the strong center-vortex-condensation criterion for $Z[B]/Z[0]$, but it isolates the remaining global normalization problem and separates a global twist from an actual dynamical vortex.

</details>


### [175] [Topics in Kadyshevsky Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03530)
*Th. A. Rijken,J. W. Wagenarr*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Kadyshevsky量子场论框架下系统整理了若干场论专题。首先构建了第二量子化的准粒子形式并发展出泛函积分形式（路径积分、Schwinger-Symanzik方程、广义Wightman函数及Kadyshevsky约化公式）；其次探讨了Feynman微扰论与Kadyshevsky微扰论的关系，并将Gross-Jackiw方法推广至含导数的相互作用拉格朗日量情形，应用于π-N相互作用（赝矢量NNπ耦合、矢量NNρ耦合及规范不变的Δ33Nπ耦合）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kadyshevsky形式的主要动机在于其图中粒子始终保持在质壳上（与Feynman图不同），这便于引入唯象形状因子（如高斯型），从而在低能强子物理中更有效地处理截断和唯象修正。

Method: 第一部分发展第二量子化的准粒子算符形式，并在此基础上建立泛函积分形式，包括路径积分、Schwinger-Symanzik方程、广义Wightman函数及Kadyshevsky约化公式。第二部分首先研究Feynman与Kadyshevsky两种微扰论之间的联系，然后将Gross-Jackiw方法移植到Kadyshevsky框架，用于处理含导数相互作用的拉格朗日量，具体应用于赝矢量NNπ、矢量NNρ以及规范不变的Δ33Nπ耦合。

Result: 文章系统性地建立了Kadyshevsky框架下的准粒子第二量子化与泛函积分体系，完成了该框架与标准Feynman微扰论的关系梳理，并将Gross-Jackiw方法成功推广至含导数耦合的强子相互作用（π-N、ρ-N及Δ-N系统）。

Conclusion: 该工作为Kadyshevsky量子场论形式提供了完整的解析工具集（泛函积分与约化公式），并展示了其在具有导数耦合的实际强子物理模型（尤其是π-N体系）中的可应用性。

Abstract: In these notes we deal with several field theoretical topics in the framework of the Quantum Field Theory as developed by Kadyshevsky. The main motivation for studying the Kadyshevsky formalism is that in the kadyshevsky graphs, in contrast with the Feynman graphs, the particles remain on the mass shell. This facilitates the use of phenomenological form factors, {\it e.g.} Gaussian ones. In the first part we construct the second quantised quasi-particle formalism, which is employed to develop the functional integral formalism. In the latter we develop the path-integral, the Schwinger-Symanzik equations, generalised Wightman functions, and the Kadyshevsky reduction formulas. In the second part we cover the topics: (i) the relation between the Feynman and Kadyshevsky perturbation theory, and (ii) the Gross-Jackiw method in the Kadyshevsky formalism. The latter method is applied to the kadyshevsky formalism for interaction Lagrangians with derivatives, in particular for pion-nucleon interactions: (i) pseudo-vector $NNπ$-, (ii) vector $NNρ$-, and (iii) gauge-invariant $Δ_{33}Nπ$ coupling.

</details>


### [176] [Quasi-local form for $α$--$z$ Rényi QNEC from fixed-ray escorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04016)
*Tanay Kibe,Pratik Roy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用固定射线伴随（fixed-ray escort）积分表示，将α-z Rényi散度表示为伴随态族上普通相对熵的平均，据此推导了伴随平均纠缠第一定律、α-z信息核的伴随平均表示，以及共形场论中球状区域的伴随平均Bekenstein型界。对于推测性的α-z量子零能量条件（QNEC），获得了准局域形式，并在自由标量场论相干态激发下显式计算了Rényi QNEC及伴随输运项，验证了该族中α-z QNEC的对角猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将α-z Rényi散度这一广义量子信息度量引入量子场论（QFT）的纠缠结构研究中，特别是挖掘其在熵不等式、信息核和QNEC等课题中的应用潜力，并为α-z QNEC猜想提供具体验证。

Method: 利用固定射线伴随积分表示（将α-z Rényi散度化为伴随态上普通相对熵的加权平均），在标准紫外正规化的子区域密度矩阵框架下推导多种量子信息量。对QNEC采用准局域化处理，引入伴随输运项修正；并在自由标量场论的相干态激发下进行显式计算。

Result: 推导出伴随平均纠缠第一定律、α-z信息核的伴随平均表示及CFT球域的伴随平均Bekenstein型界；获得α-z QNEC及Rényi QNEC（z=α特化）的准局域形式；对自由标量场相干态完成了Rényi QNEC的显式计算并给出输运项；同时验证了该相干族中α-z QNEC对角猜想和正的α-z零Hessian。

Conclusion: 固定射线伴随表示是处理一般α-z Rényi散度在QFT中应用的强大工具，可推广一系列基于相对熵的纠缠不等式和物理约束至更一般的α-z框架，并在具体算例中验证了α-z QNEC猜想的对角形式。

Abstract: The fixed-ray escort integral representation expresses $α$--$z$ Rényi divergence as an average over ordinary relative entropy of a family of escort states. Working in the standard UV-regulated density-matrix description of QFT subregions, we use this representation to derive an escort-averaged entanglement first law, an escort-averaged representation of the $α$--$z$ information kernel, and an escort-averaged Bekenstein-type bound for ball-shaped regions in conformal field theories. For the conjectural $α$--$z$ quantum null energy condition (QNEC), we obtain a quasi-local form in which the null energy is evaluated in an escort-averaged state and is corrected by an escort-transport term encoding the failure of escort formation to commute with restriction to a null-deformed region. The $z=α$ specialization gives a similar quasi-local form of the Rényi QNEC for sandwiched Rényi divergence. We explicitly compute the Rényi QNEC, including the explicit escort transport term, for coherent-state excitations in a free scalar field theory. For the same coherent family, we obtain a positive $α$--$z$ null Hessian, verifying the conjectured diagonal $α$--$z$ QNEC for this family.

</details>


### [177] [Cusped Defects: Cusp Operator Expansion, Conformal Properties and Bootstrap Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04035)
*Lorenzo Bianchi,Andrea Cavaglià,A. David Gutiérrez,Stefanos R. Kousvos,Marco Meineri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了一般共形场论（CFT）中嵌入的具有尖角（cusped）缺陷线的普遍性质，证明尖峰标度算子（cusp scaling operators）在一般共形变换下如同初级算子（primary operators）一样变换，并通过尖峰算子展开（COE）将任意形状的缺陷在尖峰缺陷基上展开。理论结果在局域磁场缺陷中得到了验证，并利用交叉方程推导了动力学尖峰数据的解析约束，讨论了数值bootstrap应用的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷线（defect lines）在共形场论中具有重要作用，但此前对含尖角（cusp）的缺陷线的系统性研究较为缺乏。理解尖峰缺陷在一般共形变换下的变换性质以及将其作为展开基的可能性，是深入理解缺陷结构的基础性问题，也为后续bootstrap方法在缺陷系统中的应用提供理论前提。

Method: 论文通过以下技术路线展开：(1) 形式化证明尖峰标度算子在一般共形变换下满足初级算子变换法则；(2) 建立尖峰算子展开（COE），表明一般形状的缺陷可分解为尖峰缺陷的叠加；(3) 以局域磁场缺陷作为具体示例对上述普遍结论进行检验；(4) 利用交叉方程（crossing equation）推导尖峰共形数据（cusp data）所满足的解析约束条件，为数值bootstrap奠定基础。

Result: 文章证明了尖峰算子的初级变换性质，建立了COE展开的完备性，并在局域磁场缺陷中得到了具体验证。此外，通过交叉方程得出了限制动力学尖峰数据的解析约束条件，展示了通向数值bootstrap的可能路径。

Conclusion: 该工作为任意形状缺陷线的共形场论研究提供了统一框架，建立了尖峰缺陷的普适变换性质和展开方案，并在此基础上通过交叉方程获得了可操作的解析约束，为后续更广泛的缺陷bootstrap数值研究开辟了道路。

Abstract: We analyze general properties of cusped defect lines embedded in generic conformal field theories (CFTs). We prove that suitably defined cusp scaling operators transform like primary operators under general conformal transformations. We show that, via the cusp operator expansion (COE), defects of general shape can be expanded in a basis of cusped defects. These findings are checked and exemplified in the case of the localized magnetic field defect. Finally, we derive analytic constraints on dynamical cusp data by making use of a crossing equation, and discuss possible numerical bootstrap applications of our results.

</details>


### [178] [Cutting corners: exciting and magical bounds from the cusp bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04041)
*Ryan A. Lanzetta,Ian Moult,Yifan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究共形场论中共形线缺陷的尖端（cusp）异常维度。利用幺正性和不同缺陷几何的切割-拼接一致性，推导了尖端异常维度的一般性下界，包括证明单态尖端最轻，以及通过矩形几何引导出引导函数得到右角裸尖端维度的最优普适下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖端异常维度刻画了共形线缺陷世界线上尖角处的动力学自由度，对理解缺陷的动力学及共形场论的结构具有重要意义。此前缺乏对尖端异常维度的一般性约束，本文旨在利用幺正性和自举方法建立这些维度的普适边界。

Method: 利用幺正性以及共形场论中不同缺陷几何之间的切割-拼接一致性条件。对共轭缺陷构成的尖端，通过级别交叉（level crossing）论证证明单态尖端是最轻的本征态；对矩形排列的线缺陷，建立类似于无自旋模自举（spinless modular bootstrap）的约束，并构造一个解析的"魔法"泛函求得最优下界。

Result: 首先证明对于共轭缺陷构成的尖端，改变尖端角度时单态尖端与任何非单态尖端之间不会发生能级交叉，从而证明了单态尖端是最轻的。其次，在矩形几何中，通过"魔法"泛函得到了右角裸尖端维度的最优且普适的下界，该下界由对应缺陷与其共轭缺陷在平直空间中的普适缺陷Casimir能表达。

Conclusion: 本文建立了尖端异常维度的一般性约束，包括单态尖端最轻的证明以及右角裸尖端维度的最优普适下界，为理解共形缺陷动力学提供了新的自举型约束工具。这些结果不仅加深了对线缺陷物理的理解，也展示了空间几何自举在缺陷系统中的应用潜力。

Abstract: An illuminating probe of the dynamics of a line defect is the global geometry of its worldline. For a conformal line defect in a conformal field theory, sharp corners in the worldline, i.e. cusps, host dynamical degrees of freedom characterized in part by a spectrum of scaling dimensions, called cusp anomalous dimensions. We present various general bounds on cusp anomalous dimensions following from unitarity and cutting-and-gluing consistency of different defect geometries. We first establish, for cusps involving conjugate defects, that level crossings upon varying the cusp angle are forbidden between the lightest singlet cusp and any non-singlet cusp, proving that singlet cusps are the lightest. Then, we study line defects arranged in a rectangular geometry, which are subject to bootstrap constraints reminiscent of the spinless modular bootstrap. We find an analytic ``magic" functional that produces an optimal and universal lower bound on the dimension of a right angle bare cusp in terms of the universal defect Casimir energy between the corresponding defect and its conjugate in flat space.

</details>


### [179] [The growth of $SO(9)$ super-representations in Type II string theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04107)
*Alessandro Georgoudis,Joseph A. Minahan,Gustav Ström,Athanasios Zoumis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了II型和I型弦理论中massive SO(9)超表示的生长规律，通过经验公式和细化配分函数计算了各能级表示的成数（level 501以内的单个表示、level 226以内的全部表示），并推导了任意表示的渐近生长公式，利用Rademacher求和获得高精度近似值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超弦理论中massive态的超表示结构及计数，揭示SO(9)超表示在弦理论中的生长规律，这是弦理论谱结构和数学物理交叉领域的重要问题。

Method: 首先直接寻找经验公式以确定每个能级出现的表示；随后在有限域上计算细化配分函数（refined partition function）以获得各表示的成数值；接着通过对细化配分函数峰值处积分构造任意表示的渐近公式；并利用Rademacher求和得到成数的高精度近似，其中每个表示的成数同时包含偶数和奇数的Rademacher项贡献。最后将相同技术推广应用于普通表示，采用精简的细化配分函数提升计算效率和简化渐近表达式。

Result: 发现成数计算可在level 501以内对单个表示、level 226以内对所有表示完成；超弦配分函数仅接收奇数Rademacher项的贡献，但任一超表示的成数同时接收偶数和奇数项的贡献；在普通表示情形下，简化的细化配分函数带来更高的计算速度和更简洁的渐近近似表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地刻画了II型和I型弦理论中massive SO(9)超表示的谱结构和计数行为，证明了Rademacher求和方法在该场景下能提供精确的近似，并揭示了超表示与普通表示在偶数/奇数项贡献中的关键差异。

Abstract: We study the growth of massive $SO(9)$ super-representations in type II and type I string theories. We do this first directly by finding an empirical formula that specifies which representations appear at any level, and then compute the multiplicities from a refined partition function evaluated over finite fields up to level 501 for individual representations, and level 226 for all representations. We then derive asymptotic formulae for the growth of any representation, which are constructed by integrating about the peaks of the refined partition function. Using a Rademacher sum we can find very accurate approximations for the multiplicities of the representations. Unlike the superstring partition function, which only receives contributions from the odd Rademacher terms, the multiplicities for any representation have contributions from both even and odd terms. Finally, we apply the same techniques to the ordinary representations, where a simplified refined partition function allows for better computational speed and simpler expressions for the asymptotic approximations.

</details>


### [180] [Boundary conditions for axionic wormholes, imaginary distance bound and KSW allowability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04137)
*Shubhashis Mallik,Neha,Gaurav Narain*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维轴子虫洞在洛伦兹迷你超空间路径积分中的行为，通过对偶标量与三形式通量表述进行分析，揭示了跨喉鞍点（引发虚虫洞）在物理轮廓提升中不相关，并推导了与边界条件参数相关的插值约束界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子虫洞在全息对偶和量子引力中具有重要意义，但其在洛伦兹路径积分框架下的精确贡献与守恒电荷求和可行性尚未完全清楚。理解虫洞鞍点的贡献结构及边界条件的影响对于揭示虚距离界的普适性至关重要。

Method: 采用双对标量与三形式通量表述分析四维轴子虫洞，针对度量边界条件进行对偶性分析；使用Picard-Lefschetz理论在lapse覆盖平面上分析鞍点的相交数；在固定通量下精确计算路径积分；引入单参数Neumann边界条件并分析其对鞍点贡献的影响；结合KSW判据验证所得界。

Result: 证明了标量与三形式表述的对偶性在任意背景（包括复数背景）下成立；Dirichlet条件下固定通量路径积分可精确求解；欧洲几里得区域中鞍点分为同侧与跨喉两类GS虫洞段，跨喉鞍点相交数为零故不贡献；渐近平直极限下该鞍点对应饱和虚距离界的虚虫洞；Neumann条件下跨喉鞍点仍对物理提升不相关；固定电荷扇区求和收敛导出对Neumann参数与边界轴子虚部的插值约束界，KSW判据给出完全一致的界。

Conclusion: 虚虫洞在物理路径积分中不贡献，这与虚距离界兼容；通过连续变化的Neumann边界条件参数可在半虫洞与完整虫洞之间插值，并对相关的边界参数施加精确的约束界，进一步验证了KSW判据与虚距离界的一致性。

Abstract: We study four-dimensional axion wormholes in the Lorentzian mini-superspace path integral using dual scalar and three-form flux formulations. We analyze this duality for generic metric boundary conditions and real lapse integration contour, and show that it holds on any background, including complex ones. With the Dirichlet condition on the metric, the fixed-flux path integral is evaluated exactly. In the Euclidean regime, saddle geometries organize into same-side and cross-throat segments of the Giddings-Strominger (GS) wormhole, depending on whether the two boundaries lie on the same or opposite sides of the throat. A Picard-Lefschetz analysis in the covering plane of lapse reveals that the cross-throat saddle has vanishing intersection number, and therefore does not contribute. In the asymptotically flat limit, it corresponds to the imaginary wormhole that saturates the imaginary distance bound (IDB). The same conclusion follows independently on the scalar side of the duality, and is confirmed by the exact amplitude. Replacing the Dirichlet condition with a one-parameter Neumann condition, the cross-throat saddle that leads to the imaginary wormholes remains irrelevant for the physical lift of the contour. This purely imaginary parameter characterizing the Neumann condition interpolates continuously between the half- and complete-wormhole geometries. Convergence of the sum over fixed-charge sectors imposes a corresponding interpolating bound not only on the imaginary part of the boundary axion but also on the parameter characterizing the Neumann boundary condition. An independent analysis based on the KSW criterion reproduces exactly the same bound, emphasizing its compatibility with the IDB.

</details>


### [181] [Baby-universe state dependence of the wormhole-induced gravi-axion mass gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04191)
*Amin Rezaei Akbarieh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧几里得虫洞对轴子质量的影响，指出虫洞的存在并不能唯一确定轴子质量，轴子质量依赖于婴儿宇宙状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得虫洞可以破坏轴子移位对称性并产生周期势，从而可能赋予轴子质量。但作者认真对待这样一个假设：仅仅存在虫洞并不意味着轴子质量被唯一确定，需要更多信息来判定其质量，因此需要对此进行深入研究。

Method: 作者首先证明了对于具有O(4)对称性的Giddings–Strominger虫洞，引力Pontryagin密度为零，因此动力学的Chern–Simons相互作用可以影响量子效应（包括行列式相位和费米子选择规则）而不会改变经典虫洞作用量。随后在固定α扇区中分析轴子质量对婴儿宇宙状态的依赖性，并处理α扇区混合情形下条件质量的分布，研究了平均谱测度的下边缘与条件质量平方的本质下确界之间的关系。最后通过高斯婴儿宇宙态的具体实例验证结论。

Result: 在固定的α扇区中，轴子可以具有特定质量，但该质量取决于婴儿宇宙状态而非普遍值。在α扇区混合的情况下，平均谱测度的下边缘由条件质量平方在α分布上的本质下确界决定。即使几乎所有个体扇区都有质量且平均质量平方非零，如果测度的支撑在对称点α=0处累积，平均谱测度将是无隙的（gapless）。以高斯婴儿宇宙态为例，谱阈值从零开始且不存在孤立的质重极。

Conclusion: 不指定婴儿宇宙状态而从虫洞预测引力轴子的质量和宇宙学并非唯一的、与状态无关的预测。

Abstract: Euclidean wormholes can break the axion shift symmetry, and when this symmetry is broken, a periodic potential is created for the axion, and the curvature of this potential at a minimum point can give the axion mass. In this work, we take seriously the assumption that the mere existence of a wormhole does not mean that the axion mass is uniquely determined, and that more information is needed to determine it. We first show that since for a Giddings--Strominger wormhole with symmetry \(O(4)\), the gravitational Pontryagin density is zero, the dynamical Chern--Simons interaction can affect quantum effects, including the determinant phase and fermionic selection rules, without changing the classical wormhole action. We also conclude that in a fixed \(α\) sector, the axion can have a specific mass, but this mass depends on the baby-universe state and is not a universal value. Then, for the mixed case of \(α\) sectors that give a distribution of conditional masses, we show that the lower edge of the averaged spectral measure is determined by the essential infimum of the conditional mass squared over the $α$ distribution. Finally, we conclude that even if almost all individual sectors are massive and the mean mass squared is also non-zero, if the support of the measure accumulates at the symmetric point \(α=0\), the averaged spectral measure will be gapless. To clarify the discussion, we consider a clear example of this situation, which can be a Gaussian baby-universe state, and show that in it the spectral threshold starts at zero and there is no isolated massive pole. Therefore, predicting the mass and cosmology of a gravi-axion from a wormhole without specifying the baby-universe state is not a unique, state-independent prediction.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Homology Nilpotency in Rational Homotopy Theory: Cell Attachments, Retractive Towers, Divergence, and Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02980)
*Paul-Eugène Parent*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究单连通极小 Sullivan 代数的同调幂零性（homology nilpotency），建立了上下界判据及有理胞腔贴附方法，构造了一个满足 nil_h(M)=3<Hnil(M)=4 的有限型极小 Sullivan 代数。通过完全协调的收缩塔（retractive towers）刻画同伦幂长度与商缺陷，并证明在逐次有限楔和稳定化后，Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) 对所有有限型单连通有理空间成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解同调幂零性与同伦幂零性之间的差异，揭示其结构来源（额外商缺陷），并寻找在稳定化条件下使二者与 Lusternik-Schnirelmann 分类（cat_o）一致的途径。

Method: 基于 Sullivan 极小模型理论，发展同调幂零性的上下界判据；引入"完全协调的收缩塔"作为结构工具来分离同伦幂长度与商缺陷；通过逐次有限楔（degreewise finite wedges of rational spheres）实现稳定化，消除该刚性缺陷。

Result: 构造了一个显式有限型极小 Sullivan 代数 M，使得同调幂零性 nil_h(M)=3，严格小于同伦幂零性 Hnil(M)=4；证明了稳定化后等式 Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) 对所有有限型单连通有理空间 X 成立。

Conclusion: 同调幂零性与同伦幂零性的差距来自收缩塔中额外的商缺陷，而该缺陷可通过逐次有限楔稳定化消除，最终导致同伦幂零性（稳定化后）与约化 Lusternik-Schnirelmann 分类 cat_o 精确一致。

Abstract: Homology nilpotency of a simply connected minimal Sullivan algebra $M$ is the least integer $n$ for which $(M^+)^{n+1}$ is contained in an acyclic differential ideal. We develop lower- and upper-bound criteria, including methods for rational cell attachments, and construct an explicit finite-type minimal Sullivan algebra $M$ satisfying $nil_h(M)=3<Hnil(M)=4$.
  Complete strictly coordinated retractive towers identify the obstruction: they characterize homotopical nil-length while exposing the additional quotient defect measured by homology nilpotency. We then show that this rigid defect disappears after stabilization by degreewise finite wedges of simply connected rational spheres: $$
  Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) $$ for every simply connected rational space $X$ of finite type.

</details>


### [183] [Generalized Telescope Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03375)
*Jiacheng Liang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入了"原子粉碎框架"（atomic smashing frame），将Balmer谱从张量三角几何推广到任意可表对称幺半∞-范畴。作者建立了粉碎理想与局部刚性局部化之间的对应关系，并证明了关于粉碎框架的recollement定理。在色同伦理论中，他们证明自然投影将Sp的粉碎框架嵌入到所有素数和高度上的单色层Sp_{T(n)}的粉碎框架乘积中。文章还刻画了∞-topos和连通模范畴中的望远镜猜想，并引入了介于原子和通常粉碎框架之间的Serre粉碎框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Balmer谱和张量三角几何的理论框架从经典的稳定紧致刚性生成情形推广到更一般的对称幺半∞-范畴设置中，从而为望远镜猜想的多个领域（包括色同伦理论、∞-topos和模范畴）提供统一的表述和工具体系。

Method: 1) 定义原子粉碎框架作为Balmer谱的推广。2) 利用dualizable和刚性∞-范畴建立粉碎理想与局部刚性局部化的对应关系。3) 在（预）稳定设置中证明recollement定理。4) 通过自然投影建立Sp的粉碎框架与Sp_{T(n)}的乘积之间的嵌入。5) 在非稳定设置中用粉碎域刻画∞-topos的望远镜猜想，用Pierce型条件刻画连通模范畴和超完备连通层。6) 引入Serre粉碎框架作为中间结构层。

Result: 成功将粉碎框架推广到任意可表对称幺半∞-范畴；建立了粉碎理想与局部刚性局部化的对应关系；证明了稳定与非稳定设置下的recollement定理；建立了Sp的粉碎框架到单色层乘积的嵌入，从而将Sp的spatiality问题化简为Sp_{T(n)}的spatiality问题；用Pierce型条件刻画了连通情形下的望远镜猜想；引入了位于原子与通常粉碎框架之间的Serre粉碎框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为望远镜猜想提供了统一的∞-范畴论框架，将稳定与非稳定设置联系起来，并指出了研究连通R-模范畴中望远镜猜想的新的中间结构层次。色同伦理论中Sp的spatiality问题被归结为各单色层Sp_{T(n)}相应问题，这为理解稳定同伦范畴的结构提供了重要途径。

Abstract: We introduce the atomic smashing frame, extending the Balmer spectrum from tensor-triangular geometry to an arbitrary presentably symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category $\mathcal{V}$. This yields a formulation of the telescope conjecture for $\mathcal{V}$ and recovers the classical Balmer spectrum in the stable compactly-rigidly generated case.
  Exploiting dualizable and rigid $\infty$-categories, we establish a correspondence between smashing ideals and locally rigid localizations. This leads to a recollement theorem for smashing frames in the (pre)stable setting, together with an atomic refinement in the stable compactly-rigidly generated case. As a major application to chromatic homotopy theory, we show that the natural projections induce an embedding of the smashing frame of $\mathrm{Sp}$ into the product of the smashing frames of the monochromatic layers $\mathrm{Sp}_{T(n)}$, over all primes and heights. In particular, the spatiality of the smashing frame of $\mathrm{Sp}$ reduces entirely to that of $\mathrm{Sp}_{T(n)}$.
  In the unstable setting, we characterize the telescope conjecture for $\infty$-topoi in terms of smashing fields, and for connective module categories and hypercomplete connective sheaves in terms of Pierce-type conditions. Finally, we introduce the Serre smashing frame. Over a connective $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-ring $R$, this frame sits between the atomic and usual smashing frames, providing an intermediate structural layer in the study of the telescope conjecture for the connective $R$-module category.

</details>


### [184] [From configuration spaces to graph complexes via FA-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04033)
*Ayako Carter,Benjamin C. Ward*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了将圆楔和的紧支撑上同调建模为多项式函子的问题，通过对FA-模的cobar构造识别了该函子的系数Φ[n,m]，并利用这些系数在图同调计算中的应用，给出了可嵌入到H_c^*(F(S^1∨S^1,n))中的图复形例子，包括对Payne-Willwacher genus 2标记图复形的新显式分解，从而描述了gr_{11}H_c^*(M_{2,n})的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gadish和Hainaut（承袭Petersen的工作）将圆楔和的紧支撑上同调建模为多项式函子，但该函子的系数尚未被明确识别。识别这些系数能够建立与图同调之间的联系，并为模空间紧支撑上同调的深入理解提供工具。

Method: 通过FA-模的cobar构造来识别多项式函子的系数Φ[n,m]；将该识别结果应用于图同调的计算，以给出可嵌入H_c^*(F(S^1∨S^1,n))的图复形例子；对Payne-Willwacher标记图复形在genus 2的情形给出用简单FA-模表示的显式分解，并将gr_{11}H_c^*(M_{2,n})描述为装饰树复形的上同调。

Result: 成功识别了函子系数Φ[n,m]并证明了其与图同调计算的形式联系；给出了包含嵌入结果的图复形例子；得到Payne-Willwacher标记图复形genus 2情形的新显式分解；将gr_{11}H_c^*(M_{2,n})描述为装饰树复形的上同调，并证明了gr_{11}H_c^{n+1}(M_{2,n})=0。

Conclusion: 该研究通过cobar构造统一了圆楔和上同调的多项式函子理论与图同调，为模空间M_{2,n}的紧支撑上同调在特定分次下的结构提供了新的计算视角和具体结果。

Abstract: Work of Gadish and Hainaut (after Petersen) models the compactly supported cohomology of a wedge of circles as a polynomial functor. We identify the coefficients of this functor, $Φ[n,m]$, via a cobar construction of $\mathbf{FA}$-modules. This identification formally implies that these coefficients will arise in computations of graph homology, and we use this result to give examples of graph complexes whose homology may be embedded in $H_c^\ast(F(S^1\vee S^1,n))$.
  This includes the Payne-Willwacher marked graph complex in genus 2, for which we give a new, explicit decomposition in terms of simple $\mathbf{FA}$-modules. This allows us to describe $\mathsf{gr}_{11}H_c^{\ast}(\mathcal{M}_{2,n})$ as the cohomology of a complex of decorated trees and to show, for example, $\mathsf{gr}_{11}H_c^{n+1}(\mathcal{M}_{2,n})=0$.

</details>
